After deciding to believe Geom Mugeuk's words, he had thought such a moment might come. The reaction of Cheonae Geosa that he had recalled then was this:

I'm sorry. I couldn't help it. Still, my friendship with you was real.

But that was truly a naive thought.

In reality, he was acting like a man he had met only yesterday. How could a person deny their acquaintance so completely in one moment?

Because of that, the emotion pressing upon him most strongly was not hatred or anger, but emptiness.

The two people's gazes intertwined in midair. They had never once quarreled in their lives, yet now they were revealing completely different energies.

Jin Paecheon entered Hwaryulcheong's battlefield. Scattering ash and piled corpses, blood flowing across the ground, screams wailing from all directions.

*Is this your true form?*

Hwaryulcheong standing alone in the middle of that battlefield—his heated gaze was all too unfamiliar.

Jin Paecheon, who had been staring at Hwaryulcheong, looked at Geom Mugeuk. If it truly hadn't been for Geom Mugeuk, he would never have uncovered this true form.

"Young Cult Leader."

"Yes, Alliance Lord."

Jin Paecheon didn't say any other lengthy words. All his emotions were contained in his gaze, so he said just this one thing.

"I am in your debt."

If it truly hadn't been for Geom Mugeuk, he would have been stabbed in the back by Cheonae Geosa someday. Because he would only have aimed for the perfect moment. Because he would have never dreamed this friend would stab him in the back.

Geom Mugeuk had saved not only his life, but the Martial Alliance and the entire orthodox murim as well. It was an enormous debt, yet Geom Mugeuk immediately tore up the debt note that could have been used to demand repayment endlessly.

"A debt? No. It is thanks to the Alliance Lord believing in me. You are the one who uncovered it, Alliance Lord."

It was the moment a vent opened in Jin Paecheon's heart, and the small lingering hole of doubt that had remained in its stead was completely filled.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Hwaryulcheong.

"You've finally revealed your true colors. How many years has it been? No, how many decades?"

Even in this moment, Hwaryulcheong maintained his composure.

His opponents were the Martial Alliance Lord and Geom Mugeuk. He should know full well how strong the Alliance Lord's martial arts were, not to mention Geom Mugeuk's.

That he could remain calm against these two people clearly meant he had his own preparations.

Geom Mugeuk kept Jin Paecheon from relaxing his guard.

"This man knew from the beginning the Alliance Lord would be here. No, he may have guessed as much before coming and arrived here anyway."

Jin Paecheon understood what Geom Mugeuk was trying to say. It meant to be careful because the opponent might have a plan.

That was something he didn't need to worry about. Because his heart had already grown cold from Hwaryulcheong's reaction.

Now, this was what Jin Paecheon was curious about.

"What purpose did you have in approaching me?"

Was he after his position? Or did he hold a personal grudge? Or perhaps he had some other intention he couldn't have imagined? He was so very curious about the reason.

But Hwaryulcheong, as if he couldn't say it, silently looked up at the sky. Blue sky was visible through the bamboo.

Then, a single word flowed from Geom Mugeuk's mouth.

"Amheukgung."

In that moment, he could see it. An earthquake occurring on Hwaryulcheong's face. He couldn't hide his surprise.

It was a reaction confirming Geom Mugeuk had hit the mark exactly.

Jin Paecheon also knew of Amheukgung's existence. Because as far as he knew, there was only one Amheukgung in murim history.

"If you speak of Amheukgung, do you mean that Amheukgung which was one of the guardian families of Cheonuigung?"

Geom Mugeuk nodded.

"Yes, that's right. Exactly the Amheukgung from three hundred years ago."

Jin Paecheon was well aware of the battle between the Martial Alliance and Cheonuigung from three hundred years ago.

The one who had annihilated the Cheonuigung, a mysterious martial faction dreaming of conquering murim, was the Martial Alliance Lord of the time, Muhwang Singeom. How could he not know the greatest achievement of the greatest alliance lord in Martial Alliance history?

Jin Paecheon's eyes blazed intensely.

The fact that Cheonuigung, annihilated three hundred years ago, had survived until now dreaming of revenge was beyond surprising—it was shocking.

At the same time, now he could understand. He had wondered what reason could allow someone to hide their identity for decades.

It must have been that this grudge had been passed down until now for the sake of revenge for the annihilated family.

He could also now clearly understand why Hwaryulcheong's energy contained such a dark aura.

Hwaryulcheong's eyes blazed intensely as well. When Geom Mugeuk had earlier called him the very incarnation of revenge, he had been half-convinced and half-doubtful.

Geom Mugeuk was so intelligent that he might have guessed as much, or so he had thought. But Geom Mugeuk knew his true identity exactly.

Hwaryulcheong looked at Geom Mugeuk and asked with his gaze.

*How on earth did he know?*

Since he couldn't say he had gone back three hundred years through the Bigwi to witness the battle directly.

"Our cult can find out anything if it wishes to know."

He put forth the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult as his answer instead.

"We have been fighting you for quite a long time. Powerful individuals like you couldn't have fallen from the sky. We expected you must certainly have a connection of enmity with the Majungsa."

Since Geom Mugeuk had been at the center of the fight against the forces behind the scenes until now, it was not strange that he had discovered this fact.

Jin Paecheon confirmed once more with Hwaryulcheong.

"Are you truly a descendant of Amheukgung?"

Jin Paecheon wanted to hear Hwaryulcheong's story. About the Amheukgung he spoke of. That he was such-and-such a person, that he had undertaken this task with such-and-such a heart.

But Hwaryulcheong only looked at Jin Paecheon with indifferent eyes, as if he had no intention of saying such things. The friendship of the past years held no meaning whatsoever in this place.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to him.

"What meaning is there in settling a grudge from three hundred years ago? What did you sacrifice your life for? For ancestors you don't even know?"

Hwaryulcheong had an expression of wanting to say something. But he didn't give the reason.

Instead, he put on a strange smile and asked Geom Mugeuk.

"You think you've won, don't you?"

A gaze that clearly held some plan.

"Did you really think I didn't know that man would come?"

Jin Paecheon felt bitter inwardly as he listened. Because he was referring to him as "that man." It seemed he was the only one who had considered them friends.

Jin Paecheon slowly walked forward, revealing an even more powerful energy.

"Yes, if you came knowing I was here, then you must have come to settle things with me."

Jin Paecheon, too, no longer had any intention of conversing with him. He was not a friend, but an assassin-like being of extraordinary patience who had infiltrated for the sake of revenge.

And Jin Paecheon resolved to kill him with his own hands.

"It is our affair, so let us be the ones to tie the knot."

Hwaryulcheong's energy was formidable as well, and since they didn't know what martial arts he possessed, this was a fight where they absolutely could not let their guard down.

Then, at that very moment, words he had never imagined flowed from Hwaryulcheong's mouth.

"If you draw that sword, Jin Hagun dies."

In an instant, Jin Paecheon's gaze turned cold.

This was the reason Hwaryulcheong had been so composed even before two absolute masters.

"You think you two created this situation today? Not at all! This is a stage I created."

It meant he had anticipated everything. In other words, this stage was Hwaryulcheong's stage.

"Great Lord Jin is being guarded by the Demon Extermination Corps warriors. Even if you want to kill him, you cannot easily."

Hwaryulcheong laughed at Geom Mugeuk's words.

"You don't feel it's dangerous because of the Demon Extermination Corps?"

Geom Mugeuk immediately understood his meaning.

"You intend to use the Soul Subjugation Art."

He meant to use the Soul Subjugation Art on the Demon Extermination Corps warriors to kill Jin Hagun.

And today, Hwaryulcheong's plan wasn't limited to just that. His true plan was this:

"If you wish to save your grandson..."

Hwaryulcheong's gaze shifted from Jin Paecheon to Geom Mugeuk.

"Kill the Young Cult Leader."

!

Jin Paecheon hadn't known he would make such a demand of him. He was one who wouldn't blink at most things, but in this moment, even Jin Paecheon was flustered.

On the other hand, Geom Mugeuk maintained his composure with an unchanging expression.

Chilled by that composure, Hwaryulcheong asked.

"Why? Do you think we can't kill Jin Hagun?"

Then Geom Mugeuk answered honestly.

"No. I think you could probably kill him."

Hwaryulcheong looked at Geom Mugeuk with an expression that truly said a rascal is a rascal.

Wasn't this a situation where he should be persuading Jin Paecheon that Jin Hagun would be safe no matter what, if they were trying to kill him using Jin Hagun's life as leverage?

"Now I see."

Geom Mugeuk naturally realized one fact. Previously, a master of the Soul Subjugation Art had shown him his skill in advance. Geom Mugeuk had told the Poison King that those people must have some intention.

"Now I understand why they showed me the Soul Subjugation Art in advance. It wasn't to show me how great their skill was, was it? It was to let me know the Soul Subjugation Art would work even on the Demon Extermination Corps."

"In your current position, shouldn't you hide that fact?"

He had naturally expected them to hide it, and had tried to use the fact that they hid it to incite conflict between Jin Paecheon and him.

"But Hagun is my friend. I couldn't do that."

Hwaryulcheong put on a strange smile and then spoke to Jin Paecheon.

"He is truly someone worth cherishing. But no matter what, you couldn't kill your grandson, could you?"

Jin Paecheon glanced at Geom Mugeuk and then asked Hwaryulcheong.

"What do you intend to do after killing the Young Cult Leader?"

"It simply becomes as if nothing happened. I will leave, and you can continue protecting the Martial Alliance with your grandchildren."

"So what do you gain?"

"Killing that Young Cult Leader who has dealt us enormous damage all this time."

Geom Mugeuk scoffed and cut in.

"Could it be for just that reason? Would someone like that hide their identity and live for decades?"

Hwaryulcheong ignored Geom Mugeuk's words and pressured Jin Paecheon.

"Don't think too hard on it. This will become a secret between only us. As long as I don't speak, no one will know you killed the Young Cult Leader for your grandson's sake. Even if the Demonic Cult claims so, you can simply dismiss it as a conspiracy."

There, Hwaryulcheong added a compelling reason.

"Think of when such an outstanding Young Cult Leader becomes the Demonic Cult Leader. If you don't kill him today, the orthodox faction will fall to him anyway. So hurry up and kill him."

While he was persuading Jin Paecheon, Geom Mugeuk simply listened.

Soon, Jin Paecheon reached a conclusion. Even with his grandson's life and the future of the orthodox faction on the line, his deliberation was not long.

"I will not kill the Young Cult Leader."

At that firm decision, Hwaryulcheong stabbed at the Alliance Lord's sore spot.

"Do you mean you cherish the Young Cult Leader more than your grandson? What would the martial artists think of you?"

"Cherish him more than Hagun! How could that be?"

"Then why are you trying to get your grandson killed?"

Why on earth would Jin Paecheon have made such a decision resigned to his grandson's death?

Jin Paecheon's gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

"I came to this place today as a spectator."

The moment he heard those words, Geom Mugeuk smiled brightly. The Alliance Lord had not forgotten the words he himself had spoken to him.

Added to that was the Alliance Lord's faith.

"I believe this stage will not end in tragedy. Because you would not have invited me to a tragedy."

Hwaryulcheong looked at Jin Paecheon with a surprised face. It was a variable. He hadn't known Jin Paecheon trusted Geom Mugeuk to this extent. The Jin Paecheon he knew was not this kind of person.

Just as he hadn't known him, he himself had not known him either.

Naturally, Hwaryulcheong's gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

*How on earth can this be?*

Geom Mugeuk calmly explained to Jin Paecheon.

"Sojeongnak said before he died. He concluded that if I died, my father would never endure it."

Geom Mugeuk was seeing through Hwaryulcheong's plan.

"They have already calculated that the Great Demonic War will occur if the Alliance Lord kills me."

Jin Paecheon nodded. Yes, if not for such a purpose, he wouldn't have retreated willingly, after hiding his identity for decades.

For the sake of today's stage, how could Geom Mugeuk have made no preparations at all?

"As you predicted, I anticipated as well."

Geom Mugeuk said to Jin Paecheon.

"Hagun will be safe."

Until he heard those words, Jin Paecheon had been extremely tense. No matter how much he trusted Geom Mugeuk, it was still his grandson's life on the line.

But Geom Mugeuk had spoken directly. That Hagun would be safe.

Geom Mugeuk spoke to Hwaryulcheong.

"Did you think I would stand by and watch you kill my friend?"

Hwaryulcheong snorted.

"I suppose you think Mabul or the Poison King will stop it."

He had even predicted who would go to help from their side.

"Yes, those two are indeed Demon Lords strong against the Soul Subjugation Art. But that is only regarding the Soul Subjugation Art cast on themselves."

Hwaryulcheong put on a meaningful smile.

"Can they truly prevent Jin Hagun's death? From an attack by a subordinate striking the Great Lord right beside him? Or perhaps Jin Hagun will take his own life?"

It was a display of how much he trusted the Soul Subjugation Art master.

Jin Paecheon tensed up again at Hwaryulcheong's confidence. Naturally, Jin Paecheon's gaze turned to Geom Mugeuk.

As had been the case until now, Geom Mugeuk did not disappoint him in this moment either.

"To save my friend..."

The meaningful smile that had been directed at Hwaryulcheong was now directed at Geom Mugeuk.

"Do you really think they went?"

Absolute Regression: Episode 657

Chapter 657: Today, None of Us Dies
-----------------------------------
“This is the place.”

At his subordinate’s words, Jin Hagun gazed at a building in the distance.

It was an abandoned manor. When the wind blew, a creaking sound came from the half-broken main gate.

They had finally found the whereabouts of Gwak Yeong’s younger brother, the ironworks craftsman, and had come to rescue him.

This mission was special to Jin Hagun.

It was a task Geom Muyang had entrusted to him in trust. Because that prideful man had given it to him with the belief that he would do it better than himself, Jin Hagun wanted all the more to accomplish it.

“The manor is occupied by beggars, but judging by their movements, they are people who have learned martial arts.”

“How many in all?”

“There are about seven or eight outside. It looked as though there were about that many inside as well.”

They were likely disguised as beggars, counting on the fact that people avoided places swarming with beggars.

“Where are the sentries positioned?”

The martial artist who had gone out scouting unfolded a sheet of paper. On it, the buildings inside the manor had been roughly drawn.

“There are men standing guard in all four directions. Here and here, and here and here.”

The scouting martial artist quickly pointed with his finger to the places where the guards were.

As the martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad had carried out countless operations, they divided into groups on their own and decided which enemies to deal with.

“There is a hostage, so take them down quietly and enter.”

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists cautiously approached the manor from all sides. Those in the lead were Jin Hagun and the men with the best lightness skill in the squad.

The instant the beggar watching the outside collapsed from a hidden weapon, the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists surged in swiftly from four directions and vaulted over the walls at the same time.

The moment Jin Hagun lightly descended below the wall, the beggar guarding that spot tried to draw a dagger from his bosom and attack.

But Jin Hagun had come down knowing he was there, so the man had no chance to shout or attack.

Sweeik.

Slash!

With a single stroke from Jin Hagun, the neck of the martial artist disguised as a beggar was severed, and he collapsed.

The beggars guarding the other places also fell all at once to the surprise attacks of the Demon Extermination Squad. They possessed fairly decent skills, but they were not skilled enough to withstand an ambush from the Demon Extermination Squad.

Thus, the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists who had crossed the walls from all sides began searching the buildings one by one.

Drawing on their practiced experience, they silently eliminated even those inside.

Now only the largest building at the center remained.

Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists quietly infiltrated that place.

Daggers flew toward the necks of the beggars guarding the corridor. The daggers lodged precisely in their throats, and before they could fall, the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists glided forward and caught them. Without a single scream or the sound of a body falling, they gradually advanced inward.

And then they reached the final door.

When they carefully opened it and entered, they found a large main hall. It was so vast that the thought naturally arose: There was such a large space inside this building?

At the center of the hall, a man sat in a chair. Seated with his head bowed deeply, he seemed to have fallen asleep, perhaps because his sleep acupoint had been pressed.

By all appearances, he had to be the one they had come to rescue.

But Jin Hagun was cautious.

Before sending a subordinate to check, he looked at one person.

He was the man who appeared the oldest among the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists who had come with them.

In truth, although he wore the uniform of the Demon Extermination Squad, he was not a martial artist belonging to it.

Hwasong, a master of the Great Righteous Soul-Reversal Art.

The Great Righteous Soul-Reversal Art was a martial art created to counter the Soul-Subjugating Art. Originally devised based on the Buddhist martial art, Calming Soul Radiant Mind Art, it was made especially to oppose the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult’s Soul-Subjugating Art.

The Murim Alliance had trained martial artists capable of using the Great Righteous Soul-Reversal Art, and Hwasong was one of the two grand masters who taught them.

In other words, among the experts belonging to the Murim Alliance, he was one of the two most skilled at breaking the Soul-Subjugating Art.

The reason Jin Hagun had come on this operation with Hwasong was because of Geom Mugeuk.

Geom Mugeuk had sent word that among the enemies this time was a master of the Soul-Subjugating Art. He had even warned them to be especially careful, as the opponent’s skill was no ordinary matter.

After receiving that message, the Demon Extermination Squad had been moving with Hwasong all along. They had expected that the enemies might use the Soul-Subjugating Art in this rescue operation as well, and indeed, that proved to be the case.

Hwasong looked around and immediately recognized the hidden secret art.

“The Soul-Strengthening Art has been deployed here.”

Hwasong explained the Soul-Strengthening Art.

“In a space where the Soul-Strengthening Art has been deployed, the Soul-Subjugating Art manifests with far greater power. Even I would not be able to break the Soul-Subjugating Art easily.”

Jin Hagun’s expression hardened. The person who had deployed the Soul-Strengthening Art was naturally a master of the Soul-Subjugating Art, which meant that he might appear here.

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists guarded all directions with Hwasong at the center. The person they absolutely had to protect was Hwasong.

Jin Hagun raised his qi and examined the surroundings, but fortunately, he sensed no sign of anyone hiding.

Ordinarily, the first step would be to check the hostage, but in a space where the Soul-Strengthening Art had been deployed, they could not move rashly.

“First, I will break the Soul-Strengthening Art.”

“Please be careful.”

With practiced hand movements, Hwasong began breaking the Soul-Strengthening Art.

He moved a vase standing on a decorative cabinet to the side. Then he removed a hanging scroll from the wall and placed it on the floor. He was not simply tossing things aside at random. He moved each item carefully, one by one.

He also took powder from his bosom and scattered it on the floor, and erased lines drawn on the wall. Beside them, he drew new lines as well.

While he did so, Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad remained tense as they guarded the surroundings.

How much time had passed like that?

Hwasong succeeded in breaking the Soul-Strengthening Art.

“It is done.”

“You have worked hard.”

Even after the Soul-Strengthening Art was broken, Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists could not feel the change.

Now everyone’s gaze turned to the sleeping man in the middle of the hall.

A man sleeping alone in a space where the Soul-Strengthening Art had been deployed. Truly, if this operation had not been a rescue mission, they would have retreated first and foremost.

But because there was a high chance he was Gwak Yeong’s younger brother, they cautiously approached him.

Hwasong took the lead with Jin Hagun and approached the sleeping man. The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists escorted him on the left and right.

Hwasong brought his palm near the man’s forehead. A blue light seemed to flow from his hand.

“The Soul-Subjugating Art has not been cast on this man.”

At that, a Demon Extermination Squad martial artist approached and lifted the man’s head. After confirming his face, the martial artist looked back at Jin Hagun and nodded once. It meant his appearance matched Gwak Yeong’s younger brother.

“Wake him.”

At Jin Hagun’s command, one martial artist pressed an acupoint and woke the man.

Although they had been told he had not been subjected to the Soul-Subjugating Art, they did not know what kind of attack he might launch once he opened his eyes, so everyone remained tense with their internal energy raised.

The man who woke from sleep looked at Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists with a startled face. The shock and fear that could be felt from him were by no means an act.

“Please spare me!”

The man squeezed his eyes shut. Having just awakened, he did not think the martial artists before him had come to rescue him. He seemed to think they were on the same side as those who had kidnapped him.

A Demon Extermination Squad martial artist asked him.

“What is your name?”

At that, the man cautiously opened his eyes and tried to read the situation.

“Your name!”

When pressed once more, the man quickly answered.

“I am Gwak Ju.”

It was certainly Gwak Yeong’s younger brother.

Only then did Jin Hagun reveal his identity to him.

“We are the Murim Alliance’s Demon Extermination Squad. At your sister’s request, we have come to rescue you.”

He deliberately mentioned Gwak Yeong.

At those words, Gwak Ju’s eyes widened, and soon he broke into a bright smile.

“Ah! Finally! Thank you, truly, thank you.”

“Let us get out of here.”

It was then, just as they turned around to take him out.

Sweeiiik.

Clang!

Jin Hagun instinctively knocked away the sword that had flown toward Gwak Ju.

He was truly startled. A Demon Extermination Squad martial artist standing right behind him had suddenly thrust his sword at Gwak Ju.

“He’s been hit by the Soul-Subjugating Art!”

Just as that martial artist tried to attack Gwak Ju again, a comrade beside him sealed his immobilizing point.

“Uuugh!”

The subordinate’s expression twisted, and a pained groan escaped his mouth. The veins on his forehead began swelling in an instant.

“No!”

Jin Hagun’s heart dropped at his urgent shout. It was just as Geom Mugeuk had told them. He had said that if one’s acupoints were sealed while under the Soul-Subjugating Art, one would die in agony.

Fortunately, they had Hwasong.

He quickly placed his palm on the martial artist’s forehead and recited an incantation. As a blue radiance flowed from his hand, the face of the martial artist who had been struggling in pain became peaceful.

When everyone was staring at him, the martial artist was flustered.

“What happened?”

He had not even realized he had been caught in the Soul-Subjugating Art. That meant the person who had cast it on him possessed tremendous skill.

Jin Hagun spoke to him calmly.

“You were just under the Soul-Subjugating Art.”

The subordinate was shocked.

If not for Hwasong, he would have died. No, if Geom Mugeuk had not sent word in advance warning them to be careful, they would have suffered a disaster here.

Jin Hagun emitted qi and examined the surroundings. But nowhere in the hall was there any sign of someone else hiding.

“Let’s get out of here at once!”

For now, leaving this place was the best course.

Just as they were about to hurry out.

Sweeik.

Once again, one of the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists swung his sword.

Paaat!

Because the ambush was too sudden, the martial artist beside him could not avoid the attack.

Blood sprayed from his arm.

Swish, swish, swish!

Clang, clang, clang!

He lunged madly at his comrade. The other martial artists subdued him.

“Kkuaaaak!”

The one who had attacked was in more urgent danger than the one who had been injured. With his acupoints sealed, his veins bulged as though they would burst, and he screamed.

Hwasong again placed his palm on his forehead and recited an incantation. There had been no sign beforehand at all; being caught in the Soul-Subjugating Art was far too natural.

Inwardly, Hwasong was shocked.

‘This should not be possible.’

If the Soul-Strengthening Art were still deployed, perhaps, but the Soul-Strengthening Art had already been broken.

Yet in such a situation, using the Soul-Subjugating Art repeatedly like this was by no means easy. Moreover, the higher the opponent’s martial realm, the harder it was to cast the Soul-Subjugating Art on them.

If the targets were martial artists of the Demon Extermination Squad, it should be an extreme difficulty. And yet he was using the Soul-Subjugating Art in succession like this? Just who was the opponent?

Before he could even release that man from the Soul-Subjugating Art, another martial artist swung his sword and this time charged toward Jin Hagun. Jin Hagun shouted without subduing him.

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

“Everyone, keep away from the person beside you.”

The Demon Extermination Squad martial artists put distance between themselves. It was enough distance to block any sudden action from beside them.

“We’re leaving this place at once!”

“Yes!”

A martial artist at the rear ran over and opened the door. In that instant, the martial artist flinched in surprise.

Beyond the opened door, instead of a corridor, there was a space identical to this room. It was a sight like looking into a mirror.

Instinctively feeling that he must not enter that place, he closed the door and opened it again.

Whoosh!

This time, flames suddenly flared up before his eyes.

Startled by the scorching heat, the martial artist hurriedly closed the door.

Jin Hagun watched it happen while dealing with his subordinate. He could tell that they had completely fallen into the enemy’s space.

“Master Hwa! Please hurry!”

Was his internal energy insufficient?

The speed at which Hwasong released the Soul-Subjugating Art began to gradually slow.

But the speed at which they were being caught in it, on the contrary, became even faster.

Clang, clang, clang!

Another fight broke out among the Demon Extermination Squad martial artists.

“Fight without sealing their internal energy!”

They had to endure without sealing their internal energy while Hwasong released the Soul-Subjugating Art. If the number of subordinates caught by the Soul-Subjugating Art continued to increase like this, an extremely dangerous situation could unfold.

Jin Hagun could understand why the righteous murim regarded the Soul-Subjugating Demon Venerable and the Poison King as the most troublesome.

Any fight was difficult for a martial artist, but facing the Soul-Subjugating Art was especially hard. Fighting one’s own allies was too mentally grueling a battle.

Another Demon Extermination Squad martial artist was caught by the Soul-Subjugating Art.

‘We have to get out of this space!’

Jin Hagun entrusted the subordinate he had been facing to another subordinate and charged toward the door himself.

Shwaaaak!

Sword qi surged from his blade.

But the moment the sword qi touched the door, it vanished like melting snow.

*If I can’t find the dispelling method, I can’t get out!*

He shouted to Hwasong.

“Master Hwa, we must dispel this space.”

Hwasong, as if unable to hear his cry, had turned his back and was not looking this way.

It was at that very moment. Someone called out to him from behind.

“Jin Hagun, leader of the Demon Annihilation Corps.”

Startled, he turned around to see Gwakju. It was not the simple, honest gaze he had seen before.

Instead of attacking him, the man spoke calmly.

“Do you know why it has come to this?”

Jin Hagun could tell. That a master of the Soul Subjugation Art was speaking to him through his mouth.

“Why?”

To that calm question, the opponent delivered startling news.

“Earlier, while dispelling the Soul Strengthening Art, Hwasong activated an even more powerful Soul Strengthening Art.”

“!”

It was an unbelievable statement, but the unfolding situation proved his words correct. Because otherwise, it made no sense for such a powerful Soul Subjugation Art to continue unbroken.

“How was that possible?”

“I embedded a new Soul Strengthening Art within the dispelling method of the Soul Strengthening Art. He himself unleashed the Blood Heaven Soul Strengthening Art that I had arranged.”

Earlier, the man had moved the flower vase, placed the scroll on the floor, sprinkled powder, erased lines, and redrawn them.

It meant that another Soul Strengthening Art had been arranged to activate within the process he had carried out to dispel the Soul Strengthening Art.

“If the Blood Heaven Soul Strengthening Art had been activated from the start, Hwasong would have sensed something strange. But in the relief of having dispelled the Soul Strengthening Art, he failed to realize a new one had been activated. I know him well.”

At those words, Jin Hagun realized. That the one unleashing this Soul Subjugation Art, just like So Jeongnak or Cheonae Geosa, had been scheming around the Murim Alliance for a long time.

Without a doubt, he had been working in the shadows around the masters of the Great True Soul-Returning Art.

“Hwasong had once dispelled this exact same Soul Strengthening Art. Twice, even.”

Jin Hagun could tell. That the one who had provided the opportunities to dispel it was none other than the one speaking to him now.

“So without a shred of doubt, he confidently used the dispelling method he had used then.”

A hint of laughter flashed across Gwakju’s eyes.

“At times, familiarity becomes the greatest trap for a human.”

Gwakju looked at Hwasong.

Hwasong stood blankly with his back turned. He was not undoing the Soul Subjugation Art. The blue light that had continuously shone from his busy hands had vanished.

Hwasong slowly turned his body toward them.

The moment they saw his changed gaze, not only Jin Hagun but all the Demon Annihilation Corps martial artists let out groans.

The only person who could help them escape this place had fallen victim to the Soul Subjugation Art. Several Demon Annihilation Corps martial artists who had been standing around to protect him also drew their swords and turned toward Jin Hagun.

Those who had not fallen to the Soul Subjugation Art could not hide their confusion.

On top of that came shocking news.

“At this very moment, the Murim Alliance Leader is likely killing the Young Leader to save you.”

“!”

Killing intent from the opponent could be felt in Gwakju’s eyes.

“The Young Leader dies by the Murim Alliance Leader’s hand, and you die here.”

It meant they would kill Jin Hagun even if the Murim Alliance Leader killed the Young Leader. Their plan to kill both Geom Mugik and Jin Hagun was proceeding to the very end.

Even though it was the worst possible news, Jin Hagun’s expression brightened instead.

“Then they must know on the other side that we’re in danger.”

Jin Hagun said to his subordinates.

“They’ll come to help us soon.”

Gwakju laughed loudly.

“You cling to vain hope.”

But Jin Hagun’s hope held absolute faith in his grandfather and Geom Mugik. His grandfather would never kill Geom Mugik, and Geom Mugik would never allow such a situation to come to pass.

“The two people I trust most in the world know I’m in crisis?”

Jin Hagun said softly to his subordinates.

“Hold out until the end. Today, none of us die!”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 658

Chapter 658: Because I Don’t Talk to Cowards
--------------------------------------------
“To think the one who will become the successor of the Murim Alliance trusts the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult the most?”

Gwak Ju burst into laughter.

Then, just as his expression suddenly seemed to return to normal—

This time, Hwasong, who had been standing elsewhere, abruptly burst out laughing.

Laughter spread like a contagion. Then the Demon Extermination Squad warriors standing beside him, already caught in the Soul-Capturing Art, laughed in succession.

Their opponent was moving freely from one person’s body to another.

Then he entered Gwak Ju’s body, standing beside Jin Hagun again, and asked with a grin.

“Do you really trust him?”

Did he really trust Geom Mugeuk?

Of course Jin Hagun trusted Geom Mugeuk. If it was Geom Mugeuk, he would lightly evade something as trivial as the fate of being killed by his grandfather.

He was the sort of man who would save a cornered friend and then make an endless fuss about it. Even though he hated making a fuss.

“He’s my friend. Of course I should trust him.”

At Jin Hagun’s unwavering answer, Gwak Ju openly sneered.

“The orthodox murim will collapse even if left alone.”

Then he shouted as if reprimanding him.

“As the commander of the Demon Extermination Squad, are you not ashamed before your subordinates?”

But none of the Demon Extermination Squad warriors were ashamed of Jin Hagun.

They knew better than anyone what kind of person Jin Hagun was, and they had watched from closer than anyone how he had met Geom Mugeuk and what sort of relationship they had formed until now.

Did he sense the loyalty in their expressions? Gwak Ju’s eyes sank coldly.

“You die here today.”

He was devising an utterly cruel death.

“After you watch your subordinates kill one another, I’ll kill you last.”

If he cut down this Gwak Ju before his eyes, would his opponent die as well?

He thought not. If that were the case, the man would not have exposed himself before him so defenselessly.

Jin Hagun quietly looked into Gwak Ju’s eyes.

There were many things he wanted to say to the master of the Soul-Capturing Art hiding beyond them.

A cowardly craven who could not even show himself!

He wanted to provoke him and mock him.

But he did not think that would make the bastard reveal himself. If he were the sort of person to do that, he would have shown himself from the start.

A clumsy provocation would only put his subordinates’ lives in even greater danger.

Yes, what was needed now was not to fall for his opponent’s provocation, but to hold out until Geom Mugeuk came to help. In the past, merely harboring such a thought would have wounded his pride, but not anymore. Saving his subordinates was more important than pride.

Jin Hagun stepped back and leveled his sword. The subordinates beside him lined up on either side of him.

To be honest, he had no idea how to deal with this man. Just what did he have to do to break this Soul-Capturing Art?

At that moment, his grandfather’s words came to mind.

Do you know why we have not collapsed before their cruel and tenacious desires?

Jin Hagun spoke proudly to his subordinates.

“The path we walk is the righteous path!”

Yes. No matter how much they hurried, they were always one step, two steps behind this bastard’s evil, but that belief did not change.

“We are the Demon Extermination Squad. We fear no enemy!”

Clack, clack, clack, clack!

At Jin Hagun’s words, his subordinates leveled their swords as one. They were aimed at the comrades who had been with them just moments ago.

Shiiiiik!

Starting with the warrior right beside Jin Hagun swinging his sword at him, the battle that had stopped began again.

Clang!

Jin Hagun lightly blocked the sword flying in from beside him. He showed no sign of surprise or panic. The mindset Jin Hagun had come up with was this.

“From this moment on, we begin live combat training with our comrades!”

It meant they were to treat their comrades caught in the Soul-Capturing Art as if they were training. Those under its control would fight as if in real combat, while they would fight as if in training.

Clang, clang, clang!

A fight broke out among the Demon Extermination Squad warriors. For now, there were still more people who had not yet fallen to the Soul-Capturing Art, so they had some leeway.

Even so, Jin Hagun poured all his mental strength into it. There was no telling when someone might suddenly turn and bring about some tragic result.

‘No matter how much stronger he has become through the activation of the Blood Heaven Soul-Strengthening Art, he must have a limit as well.’

The opponent must also be fighting while using an enormous amount of internal energy.

But the Soul-Capturing Art of the opponent, who had gained even greater power through the Blood Heaven Soul-Strengthening Art, surpassed imagination. He truly continued using the Soul-Capturing Art without tiring.

One by one, the number of warriors falling to the Soul-Capturing Art continued to increase.

Jin Hagun could tell. If he was this skilled, he could kill them in other ways as well.

But the bastard was toying with him. He wanted to see Jin Hagun suffer as his subordinates stabbed one another to death.

“Hold out! Hold out to the end!”

Someone would surely come to help them!

Clang, clang, clang, clang!

The warriors caught in the Soul-Capturing Art rushed at them as if to kill, and because this side could not kill them, the battle was unbelievably difficult despite their numerical advantage.

On top of that, the number of those caught in the Soul-Capturing Art kept growing.

“Careful!”

He leaped over a subordinate rolling on the floor and blocked another subordinate attacking him.

Clang, clang, clang!

Sparks flew, and in the meantime, the warrior who had fallen rose to his feet.

Jin Hagun had saved him, but that subordinate stabbed toward Jin Hagun’s back.

Claang!

Jin Hagun turned and deflected the sword. Jin Hagun did not get angry. Rather, he felt sorry toward his subordinate.

‘I’m sorry. I’m sorry I can’t save you.’

He had faced many crises in his life, but never had he felt so sorry toward his subordinates as he did in this moment.

And never had his yearning for strength been as intense as it was now.

‘If I survive today, I will become strong enough never to let this happen again.’

Jin Hagun threw himself toward a subordinate being hurled into the wall. Because he moved recklessly, blood sprayed from his arm. But he had no time to examine the wound.

As he pushed back the subordinate attacking the fallen one, he watched another situation as well. Both the attacker and the one being attacked were his subordinates.

That was why he, at least, had to keep his head clear. He absolutely could not let his subordinates die here. Especially not by being stabbed by a comrade’s sword.

‘Not a single one will die!’

Jin Hagun swung his sword like a madman.

The warriors who had not fallen to the Soul-Capturing Art had never seen Jin Hagun like this before. Because they knew better than anyone why he was so desperate, they too fought like mad. Then, before long, they became enemies.

It was not enough.

Jin Hagun and the remaining Demon Extermination Squad warriors were pushed back to the wall where the rear door was.

Their opponent briefly brought the fight to a lull.

Clap, clap, clap!

This time, Hwasong clapped as he walked forward. The man who should have unraveled this space for them had become the bastard’s puppet.

“Impressive! Even fighting like this, you haven’t killed a single one of your subordinates.”

Jin Hagun could not answer immediately.

“Haa, haa, haa.”

Only his rough breathing echoed there.

The Demon Extermination Squad warriors standing beside him examined Jin Hagun’s body. His entire body was already soaked with blood flowing from his arms, legs, and back.

“Are you all right?”

At the question from the subordinate beside him, Jin Hagun nodded.

At that very moment—

Shwik!

The subordinate who had asked if he was all right thrust his sword at Jin Hagun.

As he dodged the incoming sword and gripped the man’s wrist tightly, Jin Hagun spoke again.

His eyes had already lost reason, but Jin Hagun stared into his subordinate’s eyes and said,

“I’m all right.”

He swung the subordinate, who was about to attack again, and threw him toward the subordinates caught in the Soul-Capturing Art. After landing on the floor, the man did not attack again.

“The hypocrisy of you orthodox bastards is funny no matter how many times I see it.”

Despite Hwasong’s mockery, Jin Hagun stared at him and spoke calmly.

“I’m your goal anyway, aren’t I? You’ve had enough fun, so let my subordinates out now.”

Hwasong snorted.

“You’re going to play the hypocrite to the very end?”

Hwasong slowly walked toward Jin Hagun.

He came right up in front of Jin Hagun and said coldly,

“Stop acting righteous!”

Jin Hagun absolutely did not let his emotions explode.

But his opponent wanted, by any means, to provoke Jin Hagun and see his true nature.

Hwasong looked at the door behind Jin Hagun and said,

“That is a door that absolutely cannot be opened unless I open it.”

Hwasong grinned and spoke insinuatingly.

“How about it? Shall I open it just for you? If you stop pretending and say you’ll escape alone, I’ll open it.”

The subordinates beside him nodded. It meant that if he could, he should at least escape first that way.

Of course, they knew. Jin Hagun was absolutely not the sort of man who would leave them behind and escape alone. Just as they were not.

“Why are you all so incapable of being honest?”

Hwasong tapped on the door behind Jin Hagun.

“Please open it, I want to live! Righteousness or whatever can go to hell, I have to survive first! I’ll do plenty of good deeds later. Now, the first five in line get to live!”

It was right then, as his mockery continued.

Craack!

The door broke apart, and a hand shot out.

That hand seized Hwasong, who was standing in front of the door, by the throat. It happened so suddenly that Hwasong could not dodge in time.

Just as a choking scream burst out—

Puaaaang.

At that moment, Jin Hagun and the Demon Extermination Squad warriors saw it. Something pure white was being flung out of Hwasong’s body. The thing they had been unable to see until now was visible at last.

It flew like an arrow and entered Gwak Ju’s body.

Then the hand that had burst out after breaking the door released Hwasong’s neck. Hwasong retreated with a bewildered face.

At the same time it let him go—

Creeeak.

The door that supposedly could never be opened unless he opened it began to open.

Beyond the door was pitch-black darkness in which nothing could be seen.

A hand protruded from within that darkness.

There was a drawing on the hand. Black chains linked in a long line from the back of the hand up the arm.

From within the darkness, the owner of the hand walked forward.

The person who entered was a young woman.

The woman was none other than the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable, Cheongseon.

Jin Hagun was truly surprised by her appearance.

He had seen her at the last three-party meeting, but he had never imagined she would appear here.

Soon after, a thrilling shudder bloomed throughout Jin Hagun’s entire body.

It was the joy of confirming that his faith had been certain. If not Geom Mugeuk, who else would have sent her?

Yes, this was the kind of person Geom Mugeuk was. If he had not been such a person, how could he, the successor of the Demonic Cult, have become his friend?

The aura she gave off after their long separation had changed far too much. To the point he almost failed to recognize her.

Her cold eyes were exceedingly calm and deep. Her bearing was completely different from when she had first become a Demon Venerable.

Unknown patterns were drawn on her face and arms.

First, what stood out most was the drawing on her forehead.

A closed eye.

The closed eye was drawn in purple. It looked like a third eye.

On the left side of her face was a red whirlpool pattern, and on the back of her right hand was the drawing of linked chains he had seen earlier.

On the back of her left hand on the opposite side, there was a blue flame.

These drawings, which did not look ordinary even at a glance, were Soul Paintings.

Soul Paintings were special drawings that strengthened the martial arts of those who had learned the Soul-Capturing Art, and by drawing these Soul Paintings, one could display an even more powerful Soul-Capturing Art.

They served the same role as the opponent now using the Blood Heaven Soul-Strengthening Art to unleash a stronger Soul-Capturing Art.

The fact that the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had appeared with Soul Paintings drawn meant she acknowledged her opponent, and at the same time, it meant she intended to fight properly.

Jin Hagun greeted her with formal respect. Though she was young, she held the status of a Demon Venerable.

“I greet the Demon Venerable.”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable also gave a formal fist salute.

“I was a little late because I was meeting an honored person.”

An honored person? Who did she mean? Geom Mugeuk? If she had met Geom Mugeuk, she would have come with him. Then who on earth was it?

Gwak Ju spoke to her.

“I heard the news about the Demonic Cult’s new Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. You’re much younger than I thought. You crazy bitch, you nearly scared my liver out of me earlier.”

Even though he knew who his opponent was, he was not frightened at all. Rather, his eyes were filled with curiosity at her appearance.

“The previous Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable dabbled in the Dark Demonic Art.”

He had great interest in the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“There’s a rumor that the Young Cult Leader killed him. Is that true?”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable gave no answer.

“The taciturn type, are you?”

Then the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable answered with indifferent eyes.

“I don’t exchange words with cowards.”

It was mockery of him for not revealing himself and hiding in another’s body.

Jin Hagun felt refreshed. He had held himself back from saying those very words so many times.

Gwak Ju’s eyes sank coldly.

“Now then, let’s see just how skilled our young Demon Venerable is.”

The moment his words ended, the sound of ghosts crying began to rise from all around.

Heeheeheeheeheehee.

It was a wail that burrowed into a person’s mind.

The Demon Extermination Squad warriors raised their internal energy and resisted the cries. But like the manifestation of a sound art, the wailing burrowed into the warriors’ ears.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable slowly brought a finger to her lips.

“Shh!”

At that moment, the ghostly wails resounding from all directions stopped dead.

Now the surroundings grew so quiet it felt as if a real ghost might appear.

Absolute Regression, Chapter 659

Chapter 659: Do You Still Think I'm Cute?
-----------------------------------------
A terrifying silence flowed.

It truly seemed as if even the ghosts had been frightened into stopping their cries.

Though his move had been broken with a single counter, Gwak Ju instead burst into loud laughter.

Jin Hagun could tell. That laughter was not originally Gwak Ju’s voice. And if the laughter of a master of the Soul-Capturing Art was mixed in with it, what did that mean?

‘His emotions are wavering.’

Jin Hagun felt it. The first crack had begun to form in the world he had created.

Gwak Ju, who had been laughing aloud, abruptly stopped.

“Our young Demon Venerable is better than that old codger from the righteous faction over there.”

At those words, Hwasong’s face flushed red.

He was one of the two grand masters who taught the Great Orthodox Soul-Returning Art in the Murim Alliance.

He already felt deep shame over the fact that he, of all people, had fallen victim to the Soul-Capturing Art, and his opponent had dug right into that wound.

“That old man even activated the Blood Heaven Soul-Forcing Art himself.”

Just as his face was turning even redder, Jin Hagun stepped forward.

“At the very least, however, he does not hide behind someone else.”

Jin Hagun’s voice was clear and powerful. It carried the Righteous Heart Art (正心功), which blocked stray thoughts and drove away heart demons. At once, the feverish flush blooming on Hwasong’s face subsided.

“I have no face to show you, Commander Jin.”

Jin Hagun consoled him in a firm tone.

“There is no need to blame yourself. That man is someone who has been working beneath the surface around Master Hwa for a very long time. It was likely a conspiracy that began from the very first day Master Hwa taught his disciples. No one can know what he secretly fed you all this time, or what suggestions he continued planting. The arrangement of the Blood Heaven Soul-Forcing Art was also extremely cunning. Anyone would have fallen for it.”

Then Gwak Ju sneered at Jin Hagun.

“Our Commander Jin is quite busy. Flattering the Demon Venerable, comforting an old codger of the righteous faction.”

Even at his mockery, Jin Hagun did not waver in the slightest. Though this place was a world within that bastard’s sorcery, he was still the commander of the Demon Extermination Unit.

If anything, he was grateful to him. Thanks to this incident, he had realized many things.

It was not merely a resolve to become stronger.

After falling under that bastard’s sorcery, he had been insulted again and again, and had been forced to feel helpless.

Yet even throughout that process, he had tried to keep his subordinates from being hurt. At that time, he had continued thinking one thing inwardly.

My pride may be wounded. But my subordinates must not be.

Jin Hagun paid attention to this feeling within himself.

Seeing how he had changed, he felt for himself that he had matured. Yes, even if the tip of the sword wavered, the heart must not.

As he was steadying his heart like that, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable said to him,

“When it grows fully, a tiger is frightening. A serpent is not.”

How could he not know that she meant he could not deal with that serpent because he was still a tiger not yet fully grown?

Because he had not expected her to say such a thing, Jin Hagun was inwardly moved.

Jin Hagun lowered his head slightly, conveying his gratitude to her.

Gwak Ju cut in again.

“What a sight. A Demon Venerable of the Demonic Cult comforting the commander of the Demon Extermination Unit! Could anything be more bizarre?”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable slowly turned to look at him.

Shh!

Because the gesture felt as if it meant, You shut up, Gwak Ju’s expression hardened. Without saying a single word, she had turned his insides upside down.

Of course, he did not simply endure it and let it pass.

“Your Young Cult Leader died at the hands of the grandfather of the man you are trying to save. Now then, who is the serpent?”

In case there was any misunderstanding, Jin Hagun quickly stepped forward.

“Do not be deceived. That would never happen.”

Then unexpected words came from the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

“I know. If one of the two died, then, forgive my presumption, it would have been the Alliance Leader.”

For a moment, Jin Hagun flinched in surprise. From her unwavering gaze, he could tell that she truly believed that.

Jin Hagun could feel that it did not stem from rudeness, but from absolute faith in Geom Mugeuk.

‘Ah, now that I think about it, she is the first Demon Venerable of the next generation.’

Thinking of her as Geom Mugeuk’s first Demon Venerable, he could understand this absolute faith of hers.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable moved toward Gwak Ju and said,

“Ghost games should be played among ghosts.”

Gwak Ju smiled broadly as he watched her walk toward him. The emotion he felt toward this young Demon Venerable was this:

“How cute.”

After stopping, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable looked at him and smiled brightly.

“Don’t change your words later.”

“Even the way you talk is cute.”

Step, step, step, step!

The warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit who had fallen under the Soul-Capturing Art blocked the way in front of Gwak Ju. Gwak Ju watched with an expression that said, What will you do with them? Go on, show me your tricks.

The Demon Extermination Unit warriors aimed their swords at her and stepped forward.

Naturally, the one whose heart grew urgent was Jin Hagun.

They themselves had not attacked their comrades, even while prepared to be wounded, but the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable was different. There was no reason for her to show such consideration, nor could he force it upon her.

After giving a look to the Demon Extermination Unit subordinates beside him, Jin Hagun stepped forward. Following him, all the warriors of the Demon Extermination Unit stepped up as well.

“We will take care of them.”

“There is no need.”

At the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s words telling him to trust her and step back, Jin Hagun immediately withdrew his subordinates. He trusted her. He trusted Geom Mugeuk, who had sent her.

The Demon Extermination Unit warriors caught in the Soul-Capturing Art rushed at her all at once.

Shiiik! Shik! Shiiik!

Just as the Demon Extermination Unit warriors, watching with the thought that someone would surely die, were about to rush forward, Jin Hagun stretched both hands out to either side and stopped them. If you could not trust her, you should have stepped in from the beginning; if you trusted her, then you must trust her to the end.

The swirl pattern drawn on her cheek shone, and at the same time, a pure white wave of energy burst forward from her body.

Whoooooong!

The fierce gust of energy whipped everyone’s collars and hair.

Then came silence.

The Demon Extermination Unit warriors who had been swinging their swords all stopped in place. The swords thrusting toward the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had also halted.

“Huh?”

All of their expressions had returned to normal. They wore looks that asked why they were attacking a woman they had never seen before.

As if blowing it away with the wind, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had released the Soul-Capturing Art placed on them all at once. It was truly an astonishing divine feat.

Soon after, the Demon Extermination Unit warriors saw Jin Hagun standing behind the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable and understood the situation. They realized that they had fallen into the Soul-Capturing Art.

They all leaped toward Jin Hagun at once.

Jin Hagun welcomed them with joy.

“You have returned well.”

Seeing Jin Hagun drenched in blood from head to toe, they guessed what sort of situation it had been.

“Thank you, Commander.”

“Give your thanks to the Demon Venerable.”

At that, the Demon Extermination Unit warriors courteously paid their respects to her.

“Thank you.”

Without turning around, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable merely lifted one hand lightly.

Jin Hagun liked that about her. Perhaps it was all the more so because, as long as her connection with Geom Mugeuk continued, she was a Demon Venerable he would be seeing for a very long time.

Now, only Gwak Ju stood before the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable.

Even though the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had released the Soul-Capturing Art on the Demon Extermination Unit warriors in a single stroke, he was not flustered.

The moment his gaze sharpened—

Chang.

One of the Demon Extermination Unit warriors half-drew his sword.

Caught once more by the Soul-Capturing Art, he had tried to attack the warrior beside him, but before he could fully draw his sword, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable released him from the art.

Chang!

A sword being drawn from another man’s hand stopped.

It was truly a contest of Soul-Capturing Arts unlike anything seen before.

As if playing go, attack and defense, attack and defense, continued in succession.

Each time, light shone from the swirl pattern drawn on her cheek.

Just as the opponent had strengthened his Soul-Capturing Art with the Blood Heaven Soul-Forcing Art, it was clear that her soul painting was also lending her power. And each time the light flashed, the swirl pattern grew a little fainter.

In the end, the opponent gave up on casting the Soul-Capturing Art on the Demon Extermination Unit warriors.

Gwak Ju lightly tapped his palms together as if admitting defeat, giving soundless applause. He still acted composed, but he could not hide the astonishment in his eyes.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable spoke indifferently, as if tossing the words out.

“If you are this much of a coward, why did you involve yourself in something like this?”

It was not open mockery, but a question that sounded as if she were truly curious. That was why Gwak Ju’s face truly hardened, irritation flashing across it.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had never had an ordinary personality to begin with. She was ambitious, and her temper was sharp.

However, after recently rising to the position of Demon Venerable, she had focused solely on training. Since she was relatively weaker than the other Demon Venerables, how hard had she struggled to escape the evaluation that she was weak?

After doing nothing but train, she was now facing a strong enemy for the first time in a while, and her tongue was slowly beginning to loosen.

“It is my first time seeing someone hide behind a hostage.”

Of all things, the body he had entered was Gwak Ju’s.

She knew that this operation was a rescue mission to save Gwak Ju.

Gwak Ju immediately struck back.

“I hear the Young Cult Leader seated you in the position of Demon Venerable. Did you at least offer him that body of yours?”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable returned the provocation exactly as it came. It was a provocation returned with interest.

“And you? Did you offer up your courage before coming here?”

In the end, the opponent could not endure it.

Gwak Ju’s eyes sank coldly, and he took something out from his bosom.

Astonishingly, it was the counterfeit hidden weapon Jingeok.

One might have thought he had taken it out to fire at his opponent.

But Gwak Ju began bringing Jingeok beneath his own chin. He was trying to commit suicide.

“Since our Demon Venerable mocks me as a coward, I have no choice but to show you that I am not.”

He looked at Jin Hagun and grinned.

“Your objective was to save me, was it not? Your mission has failed.”

Because he could not let Gwak Yeong’s younger brother die like this, Jin Hagun’s heart grew urgent.

But he trusted the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. She knew that this man absolutely had to be saved.

Before Jingeok was triggered—

Something was unleashed first.

Clang, clang!

Before anyone knew it, something had wrapped around Gwak Ju’s body.

The black chain engraved on the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s hand had actually shot out and coiled tightly around him.

“Now, if you want to die, die!”

His fingers had not been suppressed. But Gwak Ju could not fire Jingeok. Originally, the moment Gwak Ju died, he would have been able to escape from his body.

But in a situation where he was bound by this chain, he could not.

“Soul-Locking Bind (鎖魂結)!”

The chain had completely imprisoned his soul inside Gwak Ju’s body. In the end, if Gwak Ju committed suicide, he would die with him.

Gwak Ju tried to sever the chain. But the black radiance only shone more intensely; it did not break.

“You drew a soul painting this powerful?”

His eyes widened, filled with disbelief.

“Who drew that soul painting for you?”

He was certain she had not drawn the soul painting herself. She did not deny it.

“If I had just a few more years, I would not have needed a soul painting to deal with you.”

When the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable finished speaking and pulled on the chain, Gwak Ju flew right in front of her.

She seized Gwak Ju by the throat.

He felt it. Her killing intent was not a mere threat.

“Do you intend to kill this man too?”

He threatened her with Gwak Ju’s life, but it did not work.

“Why? Am I not allowed to?”

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s other hand, the one not gripping his neck, rose into the air.

“It would be a profitable trade!”

Swaaaaak!

Before anyone could stop her, she struck down at Gwak Ju’s head with her palm without hesitation.

“Wait!”

At Gwak Ju’s urgent cry, her hand stopped. It truly stopped perilously close, right above his head.

He could tell. The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had genuinely intended to kill him.

For the first time, fear appeared in Gwak Ju’s expression.

Of course, he had absolutely no intention of dying together with this worthless hostage.

“I will leave.”

Thud.

As if surrendering, Jingeok fell from Gwak Ju’s hand to the ground.

But the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable still looked down at him with cold eyes, pondering.

Gwak Ju shouted urgently.

“This man is someone the Eldest Young Master promised to save no matter what. You must not kill him!”

Truly, to think he would end up making such a plea.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable smiled brightly like a madwoman and asked,

“Do you still think I am cute?”

The moment her words ended, the black chain disappeared and returned into her arm. The chain had become far paler than its original color.

Gwak Ju’s expression returned to normal.

The one inside his body had vanished from it in an instant.

With Gwak Ju as the last, everyone here had now escaped from the Soul-Capturing Art.

Watching the way she threatened her opponent, Jin Hagun felt as if he were looking at a witch. What would this young witch become when she grew older?

A furious voice thundered through the air. Now, he no longer hid his rage.

“How dare you open the door to my world and enter.”

As if he did not truly exist here, his voice echoed like it was reverberating through a cave.

“You mock me and threaten me?”

In an instant, the surrounding scenery changed.

Whoooooosh!

They were on top of a cliff where a fierce wind blew. Everyone stood precariously upon it. It was a cliff so high that clouds could be seen far below.

The cliff was real. If they fell from here, they would truly die.

“You should never have opened that door and entered.”

Originally, in a battle of Soul-Capturing Arts, entering another person’s world placed one at an absolute disadvantage. This was because breaking the art was far more difficult than unfolding it.

“Let us see if you can save them.”

Kwadududuk! Kwaduduk!

The cliff they stood on cracked apart, and its edges began to crumble. It was a height at which it would be difficult to survive with lightness arts.

As the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable chanted an incantation, the swirl pattern engraved on her face shone more dazzlingly than ever before.

At the very moment the light vanished—

Guuuuuuu!

A deep roar rose from below the clouds, and a massive cliff pierced through them and ascended.

Astonishingly, a cliff similar to the one they stood on had risen from below. There was no time to marvel.

“Everyone, cross over!”

At Jin Hagun’s shout, the Demon Extermination Unit warriors used lightness arts to cross to the opposite cliff. Jin Hagun tucked Gwak Ju under his arm and flew there.

Those whose lightness arts were lacking received help from other comrades, and all safely landed on the opposite cliff.

Kwaaaaaaarung!

In the meantime, the original cliff where they had been standing had completely collapsed.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable’s hair whipped in the wind.

“You say this is your world?”

From the cliff she had created and raised, she shouted in a clear, ringing voice.

“If I take it, then it is my world.”

Absolute Regression, Chapter 660

Chapter 660: She Was the Coolest Bitch I'd Ever Seen
----------------------------------------------------
The wind came rushing in.

The wind wailed like a beast, carrying his fury.

His voice, carried upon the wind!

“This is my world!”

The fierce wind seemed as though it would blow everyone off the cliff at any moment.

Facing the blowing wind, Seophonmajon spread her arms wide.

Whoooooosh.

Then all the wind surged toward her.

Facing the typhoon-like gale alone, she stood firm in place.

Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae warriors watched from the side.

Then Jinhagun saw it. Seophonmajon laughing with a wild, pale grin.

She was enjoying this moment of battle.

Whooooooooosh.

The wind blew even stronger.

The light of the whirlwind pattern engraved on her cheek grew more intense.

When Seophonmajon didn't so much as flinch against the rushing wind, it gradually began to die down.

Once the wind ceased completely, Seophonmajon spoke to Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae warriors standing atop the cliff.

“You won’t see scenery like this again, so enjoy it.”

At her words, everyone looked around. They hadn't thought to admire the view while the cliff was collapsing, but the scenery from the peak with clouds flowing below was truly a sight of absolute beauty.

A very brief gift bestowed by the martial art known as the Soul-Stealing Art.

A moment later, the surrounding landscape began to change.

Sssshhh.

The space changed back to the great hall where they had originally been.

His voice echoed through the void.

“I take back what I said about you being cute.”

Seophonmajon laughed and spoke.

“Still, my heart fluttered a bit. After nothing but being called a crazy bitch, it had been a while since someone called me cute.”

Jinhagun snorted in laughter without realizing it. He hadn't expected a demon lord to speak so freely.

Jinhagun asked her.

“Can you exist like this even without a body?”

“He is using the Body-Swapping Technique.”

The Body-Swapping Technique was a supreme martial art, a branch of the Soul-Stealing Art, that allowed one to move between human bodies.

“Unless you kill his true body, you cannot kill him.”

The meaning was that the way to break this space was to find and kill his true body.

“If he strays far from his true body, he cannot use the Body-Swapping Technique, so his true body must certainly be somewhere in this space.”

“It’s here?”

Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae warriors looked around the great hall in surprise. But there was nowhere a physical body could be hidden.

Then, a voice from the void.

“You cannot find my body.”

“What, is it such an amazing body that you’ve hidden it away so tightly?”

Seophonmajon moved her lips and recited a mantra, and a purple light flowed from her forehead.

Saaaaaa.

And then, an astonishing sight.

Sssssss.

The closed eye drawn on her forehead slowly began to open.

The Ghost Eye.

Emitting a purple radiance, the spectral eye looked around.

The moment its pupil stopped, her form soared through the void.

Whoooom.

She tore through space with her bare hands as though ripping fabric.

As the void tore open, what lay beyond was revealed.

In that darkness black as ink, an old man floated in the void with his eyes closed.

Fwoosh.

Her palm strike, infused with inner power, flew toward the old man’s head. Without a moment’s hesitation, she tried to kill him.

At that very moment, the old man’s eyes snapped open.

When her palm strike connected, the old man was no longer there.

Her fist merely struck within the pitch-black darkness.

Boom!

The old man stepped down onto the floor with a loud stomp. Turning with his back to them, he spoke in a low voice.

“To think you would even use the Ghost Eye?”

He couldn't believe it. The Ghost Eye was not a martial art merely used to find hidden things. It was a supreme martial art that uncovered truths hidden within the Soul-Stealing Art.

Even when he had seen the closed eye on her forehead, he had doubted it. Merely receiving the mark was no easy feat, and even if one did, it was not a technique that could be used so easily.

As Seophonmajon turned back toward the old man, the torn void mended itself, returning to its original form.

Her third eye slowly closed and soon disappeared. It was a soul manifestation that could be used only once.

The old man turned toward her and the Myeolmadae warriors.

An old man with a kindly appearance, the bearing of a benevolent elder. A face one would never expect to meet in a world of the Soul-Stealing Art.

“You are?”

The first to recognize the old man was Hwasong. At first, he thought he was seeing things. This was not someone one would meet in such a place.

But he was certain.

“Master Jin!”

Jinhyu.

He was one of the two grand masters who taught the Grand True Spirit Return Art.

He was someone known to both Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae warriors. In other words, the very Master Jin known as a good man was the one who had been trying to kill them all this time.

Only then did Jinhagun understand.

That Sojeongrak, who used poison, and Jinhyu, who used the Soul-Stealing Art, centered around Cheonaegeosa, had infiltrated deeply into the Martial Alliance. No, it wasn't infiltration. They had lived alongside the Martial Alliance all this time.

If their scheme had not been revealed this time?

If these three had orchestrated a decisive plot? They alone were fatal enough, but what if they had drawn in another enemy as well?

The result was made clear by the chill running down Jinhagun's spine.

Jinhyu's gaze was ice-cold. The presence he now carried within his body was incomparably more intense than before.

Jinhagun was bewildered. It felt so overpowering that he wondered if Seophonmajon could truly stand against him.

“Why didn’t you fight using your own body from the start?”

Then Jinhyu answered.

“For the likes of you, I didn't need to reveal my true strength.”

He smiled with composure. But Seophonmajon shattered that composure to pieces.

“Bullshit.”

In that instant, Jinhyu’s eyes twitched.

“It’s because you’re afraid to die.”

At her words, Jinhagun understood. That if he died fighting in his true body, he would truly die.

The reason he had begged so desperately to escape when caught by Seophonmajon’s Soulshackling Chain earlier had also been because he feared death.

“A coward to the very end.”

Jinhyu said coldly in response to Jinhagun’s words.

“Lord Jin, watch your tongue!”

Who do you think you're talking to right now!

Jinhagun was truly speechless. With nothing but curses on the tip of his tongue, he quietly brought his finger to his lips, just as Seophonmajon had done.

Shhh.

At that gesture telling him to shut up, Jinhyu’s rage exploded.

Swoosh.

He charged in an instant, trying to ruthlessly strike Jinhagun's cheek.

But he couldn't reach him.

Clank.

When Jinhyu looked down, chains were already wrapped around his body.

It was the Soulshackling Chain unleashed from Seophonmajon's right hand.

Jinhyu’s gaze whipped toward her.

Seophonmajon wrapped the chain once around her wrist with a clank and said,

“Trash ought to fight this crazy bitch.”

Killing intent erupted from Jinhyu’s eyes.

“You two are truly a pair!”

Jinhyu grabbed the chains and pulled them toward himself.

Clank! Craaaack!

The chains pulled taut. Light erupted from the whirlwind pattern on her face.

Blue light began to emanate from the surrounding space as well.

Hwasong sighed at that sight.

‘The Blood Heaven Soul-Strengthening Art is properly exerting its power.’

In terms of strengthening the Soul-Stealing Art, this Blood Heaven Soul-Strengthening Art possessed effects incomparable to a soul manifestation. It required far longer preparation, and an enormous amount of money must have been spent on its materials.

Since he had activated it himself, his feelings were beyond description.

Sure enough, Seophonmajon began to be slowly pulled in.

Then the flame pattern on the back of Seophonmajon's left hand flared.

Real flames arose and spread along the chains toward Jinhyu.

Bound by the chains, Jinhyu couldn't evade.

Fwoooosh.

Jinhyu’s body burst into flames.

The Myeolmadae warriors watching were of one mind.

‘Burn to death!’

The next moment!

Swoooosh.

Blue energy erupted from Jinhyu’s body.

Boom!

A metallic sound of something snapping burst forth.

Clank! Clank!

The chains that had bound him were broken.

Enveloped in flames, Jinhyu walked out.

The blue light surrounding his body seemed to shine even brighter before the flames gradually died out.

Jinhyu smoothed his furrowed brows.

“You crazy bitch! That was hot!”

Despite the unfavorable situation, Seophonmajon wasn't overawed. She was far better at swearing anyway.

“The trash bastard doesn't burn well.”

Jinhyu charged at her with killing intent.

When the two had closed to a distance where they were nearly entangled.

Shwik!

Blade-like tentacles sprouting from Jinhyu’s back flew toward her heart.

Grab.

Seophonmajon caught it with her bare hand.

The tentacle squirmed, trying to escape her grip.

Just as a second tentacle sprouted from his back.

Shhhhhhik.

A blade of sword qi swept across Jinhyu.

Blood flowed down his face as he was knocked back with a thud. Blood streamed down from the long gash on his face.

It was sword qi unleashed by Jinhagun.

“That trash is our trash. We'll be the ones to take it out.”

Led by Jinhagun, the Myeolmadae warriors rushed at him all at once.

Chaaaaaa.

A transparent barrier blocked their path.

Thud.

Jinhagun, who had been bounced back, charged again and thrust his sword into the barrier. He raised his internal energy to the limit, trying to tear it.

The other Myeolmadae warriors also threw themselves at the barrier.

Then invisible energy shot out from the barrier like awls, stabbing at Jinhagun. It wasn't just one. Dozens flew out like a hedgehog's quills.

Jinhagun raised his protective astral qi, but the awl-like energy inflicted terrible pain.

But he didn't give up on tearing the barrier.

Shreeeeek.

Just as the barrier began to tear and Jinhyu’s attention turned toward him.

Puaaaaak!

In one breath, Seophonmajon yanked the tentacle and ripped it out as though tearing it free. She had not missed the moment when his strength faltered for an instant.

Paaaaak!

Blood burst from Jinhyu’s back, and a monster’s howl rang out.

“This crazy bitch!”

When Jinhyu rushed in and tried to tear her arm off, Seophonmajon’s shadow rose and lunged at him.

Shaaaaaaalak!

Seophonmajon's shadow wrapped around Jinhyu’s face as if alive, strangling him.

Not missing that moment, her fist flew toward Jinhyu’s stomach.

Thud! Boom!

The fist was repelled by the blue light surrounding him with a metallic sound.

“Uwaaaaack!”

With extreme force, Jinhyu also tore the shadow apart with his bare hands. Seophonmajon was being pushed back in strength.

In that time, Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae broke through the barrier.

Shwik! Shwik! Shik!

The moment sword qi poured down upon him.

In an instant, the surroundings changed.

Splosh!

Jinhagun and the Myeolmadae warriors all fell into the sea. Swimming and looking around, they were in the middle of the ocean. Only the horizon was visible in the distance, a vast expanse of sea without a single rock islet.

“Where is Seophonmajon?”

Someone spoke from behind at Jinhagun’s shout.

“I am right here.”

When they turned, she was standing on the water behind them.

She wasn't floating using lightness arts; because this was the world of the Soul-Stealing Art, she simply stood there as easily as if on land.

But the Myeolmadae warriors couldn't do that. To them, this place was truly underwater.

“Ugh!”

Right then, one Myeolmadae warrior was sucked down into the water.

It wasn't just him. The other warriors were pulled in as well.

From within the water, water fiends appeared wielding weapons. They had human shapes, but they were bizarre beings covered in scales and gills.

An unknown force steadily pulled the Myeolmadae warriors downward.

The Myeolmadae warriors swung their swords underwater and fought them.

But fighting in water was absolutely disadvantageous. Internal energy consumption was far more severe, and their power couldn't reach its full potential.

Conversely, the water fiends' attacks were much faster and stronger.

Jinhagun looked around while fighting underwater. The situation was not good.

An unknown force kept pulling at them, making it impossible to rise above the water. They held their breath and endured while fighting, but just as they were reaching their limit.

This time, on the contrary, everyone was pulled by an unknown force and flew up out of the water.

Chwaaaaak.

Both the Myeolmadae warriors and the water fiends soared into the air.

“Now! Kill them all!”

At Jinhagun's command, the Myeolmadae warriors attacked all at once. If they were still underwater, perhaps, but with the battle outside the water, how could water fiends stand against the Myeolmadae?

The water fiends were all severed by the Myeolmadae warriors' sword qi.

Splosh! Splosh!

Corpses and the Myeolmadae warriors alike plunged back down into the water.

Everyone could tell.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable had drawn them back out.

“Thank you.”

At Jin Hagun’s gratitude, the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable smiled and said,

“Fighting is a goddamn mess, isn’t it?”

If it had been anyone else, he might have answered that this accursed soul-capturing art truly was. But he did not want to say such a thing to the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable. This fight alongside her did not feel that way.

“No.”

At that, she gazed quietly at Jin Hagun, then smiled and said,

“Our Squad Leader Jin is far too proper.”

A compliment from the Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable? She truly was a woman he could not get a handle on.

It was right then.

“Look over there.”

At the shout of a Demon Annihilation Squad warrior, everyone’s gazes turned toward the corpses of the water-men.

Pop, pop, pop!

The corpses burst apart like bubbles.

And in their place, new water-men were formed.

Jin Hagun’s and the Demon Annihilation Squad’s expressions hardened. Against an opponent like this, their internal energy would be exhausted first.

The Soul-Capturing Demon Venerable showed no particular change in expression, as though she had already expected it.

And that was not all.

Swaaaaaaaaaa.
