“They say if coincidences keep happening, they’re not coincidences. We meet again.”

Then Cha Iran frankly revealed the truth about this meeting.

“This time, it’s not a coincidence.”

It probably wasn’t last time either.

“I saw you enter here and followed you in.”

“Why?”

“To scout what my rival is buying.”

Cha Iran greeted Geom Muguk with a bewitching glance.

“And to have that meal we couldn’t have.”

She was frank.

To her blatant display of affection, Ian showed an unexpected reaction.

“May I ask you a favor?”

“Go ahead.”

*Please back off from my guard! Instead—*

“Please help me pick out my clothes.”

At the unexpected request, Cha Iran showed an intrigued expression.

“That’s not exactly a request one makes of a rival.”

“Asking to share a meal isn’t something one says to a rival’s bodyguard either.”

Cha Iran smiled refreshingly as if conceding the point.

“True enough.”

Laughter suited her well; however she smiled, she was captivating.

“Very well, I’ll pick out an outfit for you.”

She looked over the clothes hanging inside.

First, she eliminated the clothes Ian had initially chosen.

“No matter how carefully the artisan stitched it, this dress doesn’t suit you, young lady.”

She even explained the reason.

“You have a pure face; if your clothes are pure as well, it becomes bland and boring. That outfit over there would suit you better.”

She chose a splendid red palace dress hanging on the opposite side. It was far more revealing and much more ornate than the white palace dress from earlier.

Ian, who had briefly hesitated between the two garments, spoke to the middle-aged woman.

“I’ll take both.”

She even explained why she was buying both.

“Even if I believe Miss Cha’s words, I have to buy both, and even if I don’t believe her, I have to buy both.”

“What do you mean?”

“If Miss Cha is right, this white palace dress would suit you, so I must buy it; and if your words were a lie, it would suit me, so I must buy it.”

Cha Iran laughed brightly. It was a laugh that made one wonder how anyone could think of assassins when seeing a woman smile like that.

Ian sent a telepathic message to Geom Muguk.

“—I had no choice, considering the momentum.”

“—I’m deducting one set from your monthly salary.”

“—No way! I can’t walk around wearing a year’s wages on my body!”

The middle-aged woman packaged the clothes to fit Ian’s measurements, and Geom Muguk paid for them.

Having bought the clothes, the three of them left the place.

Cha Iran spoke to Geom Muguk.

“Let’s eat together. You promised, didn’t you?”

Geom Muguk refused with a slight smile.

“I have never made such a promise.”

“Just for a moment.”

She looked at Ian.

“I cannot. Even if the young lady permits it, I cannot.”

At Geom Muguk’s firm words, Cha Iran said with a smile.

“This just makes me want to be with you more.”

Ian smiled and then bid her farewell.

“I suppose I should have yielded the white dress to you.”

Leaving behind the remark that Cha Iran would have to become even prettier to tempt her guard, Ian walked away.

Geom Muguk followed behind her, carrying the clothes in both hands.

Watching their retreating figures, Cha Iran smiled.

“Cute.”

Cha Iran raised her head to look at the sky.

As her expression turned blank, she looked like a different woman. Her clear, cold gaze; the bright allure transformed into a dark demonic nature.

“No matter what clothes you wear, those who are to die will die.”

* * *

The road back to the inn was one of regret.

“I’m ruined! I’m someone who even savors every meal when I buy food. Ah! Style is fleeting, but debt is eternal.”

“And it wasn’t even that stylish.”

“I can’t do this. I’ll go return it right now.”

“That elegant, pleasant-faced owner said so earlier when we paid. I’m sorry, but we don’t accept returns!”

“And she didn’t even covet the white dress!”

Ian, who had been agonizing, brightened.

“If it’s come to this, there’s only one way.”

“Right, thirty thousand taels!”

Shouting about the one big gamble of their lives, the two returned to the inn.

Unforeseen guests were waiting at the inn.

They were none other than the Young Pavilion Lord An Hupyeong and Sachu.

“They’ve arrived.”

At Sachu’s words, An Hupyeong, who had been sitting with his back turned, lowered his voice gracefully without looking back.

“You are the first woman to make me wait like this.”

Chapter 690: Is Saying You Look Like Your Son Something to Get Angry About?
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
An Hupyeong rose from his seat and slowly turned around.

As dashingly and leisurely as possible.

“I am Shinhwa Bang’s…”

The moment he looked at Ian, An Hupyeong was struck dumb.

Two eyes gazing at him from above the veil.

Eyes that sucked in the soul—where in the world could such eyes exist?

They were right here.

He felt his mind go blank, and his heart began to pound violently.

From that pounding came a natural resolution.

‘I will make this woman mine, no matter what.’

It was such a clamorous resolve that even Geom Mugeuk and Ian, facing him, could sense it.

“Who are you?”

Only at Ian’s question did An Hupyeong come back to his senses and finish introducing himself.

“I am An Hupyeong, the young master of Shinhwa Bang.”

He spoke with confidence, but the reaction he had expected did not come.

Ian merely continued to stare at him.

“I have introduced myself.”

Since that meant she should introduce herself as well, Ian spoke in a low voice.

“You seem to have come here knowing who I am.”

“Of course.”

An Hupyeong showed no fear at all, despite the fact that the other party was a demonic practitioner.

To him, who had never truly experienced demonic practitioners before, the Demonic Cult was an existence in some faraway world beyond the greenhouse.

“What business do you have with me?”

An Hupyeong was having an unfamiliar experience. No woman who knew his status had ever treated him so coldly.

‘Is this what women of the Demonic Cult are like?’

An Hupyeong had fallen for this woman at first sight. It had been truly a long time since he had felt his heart race like this.

“Please, sit for a moment.”

Ian waited for Geom Mugeuk’s sound transmission, but he said nothing. It meant she should handle it herself.

—This matter must be connected to Shinhwa Bang too, right?

Still, he answered Ian’s sound transmission.

—It’s not just connected. They’re probably involved very deeply.

In a fight like this, the side with more information was ultimately at an advantage.

—Let’s use him to find out what’s going on inside Shinhwa Bang.

Ian sat down across from An Hupyeong.

When Geom Mugeuk stood behind her, An Hupyeong finally became aware of his presence.

Amazingly, he had been looking only at Ian.

With such feelings in his heart, there was no way this young and handsome guard would be to his liking. An Hupyeong glared coldly at Geom Mugeuk, and Geom Mugeuk remained calm, pretending not to notice.

Watching that reaction, Sachu found the situation familiar.

That composure—where had he felt it before?

For a mere guard to be so relaxed… The moment his thoughts reached that point, Sachu flinched.

That handsome bastard who had been wearing a veil!

Sachu recalled the Extreme Evil Young Demon. That bastard had also stood behind the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord with that same relaxed air.

Meanwhile, An Hupyeong could not take his eyes off Ian.

“I have roamed the Central Plains and met many martial artists of the Divine Cult, but I believe this is the first time I have met someone as beautiful as you.”

“Whom from our cult have you met?”

Because he had not expected such a direct question, An Hupyeong was inwardly flustered. What demonic practitioner did he know? In the end, the person he dredged up from his basket of hearsay was—

“I have met a Demon Venerable before.”

As a boast, this was not, in the end, a lie. He truly had met a Demon Venerable without realizing it. He had even warned him that if he laughed again, he would die.

Before the woman sitting before him could ask which Demon Venerable he had met, An Hupyeong quickly added,

“Ah, and last time, I also briefly met the Demon Lord. There was something we had to cooperate on regarding our sect’s business.”

The only names he had heard from the Demonic Cult were Demon Venerable and Demon Lord. Still, compared to a Demon Venerable, the Demon Lord felt easier to mention.

Ian smiled faintly. He mistook this obvious sneer in this way.

‘Yes, if I say I know a Demon Venerable and the Demon Lord, how could you keep acting arrogant? Now you see me differently, don’t you?’

An Hupyeong even asked this question.

“Have you ever seen the Demon Lord?”

When Ian only continued smiling, he grew even more excited as he spoke.

“You probably haven’t. He is an extremely busy person. In fact, even I only saw him for a very brief moment. The next time he comes to Anhui, I am supposed to take him somewhere nice, so let us go see him together then.”

While putting on this absurd display of bravado, An Hupyeong offered Ian wine.

“Now, accept a cup from me.”

“No, thank you.”

Ian declined, but he filled the cup in front of her as he pleased.

“Since ancient times, when people first meet, doesn’t a cup of wine make things more comfortable? Come, let us have a drink.”

“I don’t drink wine given by just anyone.”

“Just anyone?”

“I know nothing about you. Not even whether you truly are the young master of Shinhwa Bang.”

At that, An Hupyeong spoke as if he had been waiting for the chance.

“Would you like to go see our sect?”

“I just came back from taking the preliminaries there today.”

“Not there. Would you not like to see the stage where the finals will be held? If you are lucky, you may have a chance to meet and greet one of the judges.”

Listening, Sachu frowned. He knew the person An Hupyeong was referring to was the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord.

He was going to take her there just to look good in front of a woman? His leader was more than capable of committing such a ridiculous act.

‘Not there!’

That guest hall was truly a place he never wanted to visit again.

“Let us go. My lady, you absolutely will not regret it.”

Ian rose from her seat and pondered for a moment.

An Hupyeong, who had never permitted a woman to look down on him, was now simply waiting to hear what answer would come.

“I’ll change my clothes and come down.”

With those words, Ian went up to the second floor.

An Hupyeong smiled with satisfaction.

‘Done! She’s fallen for it!’

Yes, no matter how lofty she pretended to be, what of it? Would she refuse when the young master of Shinhwa Bang asked her to come?

At that moment, his eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s as he followed Ian upstairs.

Ignoring the fierce glare aimed at him, Geom Mugeuk also went up to the second floor.

“That bastard beside the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord, and now this one too. Why are such unpleasant bastards always attached to them?”

If you sensed that, then you should not go near them!

Sachu forced himself to hold back the words he wanted to say.

Perhaps because it had made him recall the man guarding the Cheonhwa Pavilion Lord, Sachu did not have a good feeling about the man and woman just now.

“You must be careful. We do not even know what position that woman holds within the Demonic Cult.”

“Why? Do you think she is hiding some tremendous skill?”

The problem was that he could not know. In such cases, it was usually one of two possibilities. Either her skill was nothing special, so he could not perceive it, or she was far stronger than he was.

It was unlikely that such a young woman was far stronger than him. Moreover, the young guard standing behind her also appeared to have insignificant skill.

And yet, why did this feel so unpleasant?

There was a reason An Hupyeong was certain Ian was not an expert.

“With that face, how could her skills be good? If you had a face like that, would you spend your time training in martial arts?”

That was not the only reason born from his desire for her not to be an extraordinary woman.

“Would a woman of great status enter a competition like this? Stop worrying about useless things and tell the maids to clean my room properly. Tell them to prepare wine and food as well.”

While he was swelling with a dream that could never come true, Ian was asking Geom Mugeuk for his opinion.

“It’s all right for us to go, isn’t it?”

“You already decided, didn’t you?”

“I’m asking because I’m afraid it might be the wrong decision. Ah! Making decisions is the hardest thing in the world! From now on, young master, you make all the decisions for me. If you tell me to beat someone up, I’ll beat them up, and if you say, ‘Kill them and bury them!’ I’ll bury them!”

“My lady, I am merely a guard.”

“That’s my life you’re talking about! Give it back! Give me back my life!”

She was acting overly dramatic for no reason, but in truth, Ian had already made up her mind firmly.

She was joking because the other person was Geom Mugeuk. She knew very well how dangerous this mission was.

This matter involved a plot in which an assassin she could not detect was trying to kill the leader of the Evil Dao Alliance. It was an affair for which a prophecy had appeared, showing Geom Mugeuk stabbed in the chest by the Extreme Evil Young Demon’s dagger.

Ian placed the white palace dress among the two outfits Geom Mugeuk had brought onto the bed.

If she could stop that from happening, she could go up on stage not merely in this palace dress, but naked.

“Are you really going to change into that and go?”

“If I’m going to do it, I should do it properly.”

While she changed, Geom Mugeuk waited in front of the door.

A moment later, the door opened, and Ian came out.

Dressed in a snow-white palace dress, Ian walked out with an embarrassed expression. It was an outfit that should not have been worn with a sword, but because she was wearing one, it had a distinctive charm.

Ian’s heart pounded, wondering how Geom Mugeuk would react.

“That woman lied.”

“Yes?”

“That a pure face paired with pure clothes would be dull and tiresome? Ian, you look twice as pure.”

Ian smiled happily. Hearing those words from Geom Mugeuk alone made her far happier than being told she was beautiful by thousands.

“I’m going to become rich.”

“Yes?”

“In a competition like this, there must be a gambling den where people bet on who will become the number-one beauty under heaven, right? I’m going to bet all my fortune on you.”

“What if you lose it all?”

“You’ll have to work under me for the rest of your life to pay it back. And pay back the cost of the clothes too.”

“Young Cult Leader, you’re the one who lost it, so why would I pay it back?”

The two looked at each other and laughed.

In truth, it was worth betting everything. She still had three steps left.

Wearing the red palace dress, removing the veil, and then adding makeup on top of that.

“Thanks to a certain someone, I’m dreaming big.”

“I didn’t make you beautiful. I merely restored you to how you originally were.”

As she descended the stairs, Ian briefly imagined herself in such a scene.

Her own figure, traveling through a peaceful murim together with Geom Mugeuk.

A peaceful jianghu without forces lurking behind them or conspiracies. Laughing, talking, and exchanging silly words.

She wanted to walk hand in hand with him, talking about the past.

‘Yes. For that to happen, I have to turn all these things into the past. Into a past we can speak of with a smile.’

At the sight of Ian in the white palace dress, An Hupyeong stared at her with his mouth hanging open.

As they left the inn and walked, An Hupyeong was truly in a good mood. Walking side by side with such a beauty made him feel as if he had the entire world.

As they walked, Ian asked,

“There is something I’m curious about.”

“Please ask.”

“Why is Shinhwa Bang holding this competition?”

“Why, to discover beauties like you, Lady Yi, and to raise our sect’s reputation.”

Ian stopped walking, stared at him, and said,

“I’m curious about the real reason, not something like that. You know the real reason, don’t you?”

When she asked like that, he could not bring himself to say he did not know.

But he could not tell her. Because he himself did not know the reason.

“I am curious about that real reason.”

Listening, Geom Mugeuk thought Ian was doing very well.

An Hupyeong’s face was already showing impatience. A desire to find out by any means and tell her proudly.

The place they arrived at like that was the stage where the finals would be held.

“This is the stage where the finals will take place.”

The stage had been completed, and behind the seats for distinguished guests, there was space for countless people to watch.

They were all looking around when—

An Hupyeong, who had been staring at Ian, suddenly shouted,

“I will make you the number-one beauty under heaven!”

Ian looked at him. She had to control her expression to keep from laughing. It was because Geom Mugeuk had sent her this sound transmission.

—That takes care of the thirty thousand nyang!

Sachu, listening, was dumbfounded. Just how long had he known her? On top of that, he had not even seen her face without the veil yet.

If he was that fond of women, one would think he would judge them more coolly. Then again, he was still too young for that. Yes, this was exactly An Hupyeong.

Ian looked at him and asked,

“Can you take responsibility for those words?”

An Hupyeong answered with full confidence.

“I am a man who always keeps his promises.”

* * *

There was someone watching them.

The person standing on the roof with his arms crossed was none other than the head of Shinhwa Bang, An Cheonggwang.

Just as his expression naturally twisted at his son’s absurd promise—

Saaak.

He heard the sound of flesh splitting, a sound that should never, at least, have come from his own throat.

Drip.

Something hot flowed down his neck.

When An Cheonggwang touched his neck, blood stained his hand.

Only then did he see the dagger held by the hand wrapped around his neck.

The hand gripping that dagger was fair and white.

Ordinarily, he should have died with blood spurting from his neck.

But the wound was shallow.

It had not been a cut meant to kill.

Then a woman’s voice sounded from behind him.

“Because he’s your child, you can’t even kill him. Truly, it isn’t easy, is it?”

The person who appeared from behind him was Cha Iran.

The one who had cut An Cheonggwang’s neck with a dagger was none other than her.

“Shall I kill him for you?”

An Cheonggwang looked at her with indifferent eyes. Despite his fiery temper, he let the prank of having his throat cut pass.

“You should simply keep your promise properly.”

Cha Iran smiled as she answered.

“Father and son both do love promises, don’t they?”

An Cheonggwang’s gaze turned back toward his son.

“There was no mention from the start that the Demonic Cult would become involved in this matter.”

“Unexpected things always happen. Did you ever think you would do something like this?”

Yes, even he had not known. That he would come face to face with a life like this.

But perhaps he had vaguely thought that a day like this would come someday.

Considering it had taken him only half a gak to make this decision that would change his entire life. It had taken only a little longer than deciding what to eat for a meal.

“Who is that woman from the Demonic Cult?”

“Blood really doesn’t lie.”

Knowing she meant he resembled his son, An Cheonggwang’s expression turned cold.

“Is being told you resemble your son something to get so angry about? I meant your question was wrong.”

An Cheonggwang soon understood what she was trying to say. Certainly, he was different from his son.

“Who is that man?”

At the proper question, Cha Iran looked at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Someone you cannot handle. Someone whose true identity even the Evil Dao Alliance, to which you devoted your loyalty for your entire life, would not tell you.”

She smiled brightly and added,

“Did you think that after joining hands with a demon, a devil would not come?”

Chapter 691: You Wouldn't Stay Away Just Because It's Dangerous, Would You?
---------------------------------------------------------------------------
“Why not kill such an extraordinary devil?”

An Cheonggwang was curious. He knew full well that if this woman set her mind on killing someone, there was no one she could not kill. Just as he himself had nearly died moments ago.

“There are many kinds of devils. There are terrifying devils, cruel devils, devils who toy with people until they break. What kind of devil do you think that one is?”

He had thought it would be a devil even more frightening than the ones she had mentioned.

“A kind devil.”

An Cheonggwang thought she was joking. But it was no joke.

“When devils and evil spirits get mixed together and fight, they start feeling kinship, go easy on each other, even share what they devour. You know what I mean. You must have lived that way too. But that kind devil has none of that. He just chops them off clean. If he were merely kind, we could make use of him and be done with it, but the problem is that he’s a devil. His kindness is a problem, and the fact that he’s a devil is a problem.”

An Cheonggwang sensed that this woman feared that young man.

A young master of the Demon Cult who made this woman afraid.

Had such a person truly existed?

At that very moment, one person came to An Cheonggwang’s mind. Someone he had ruled out from the start because he thought there was no way he could be here.

“The Demon Cult’s Young Cult Leader?”

To his disbelieving question came an answer that should not have come.

“I take back what I said about you resembling your son. You are a very clever man.”

Only now did he understand. Why the Sado Alliance had not given them the information. A kind devil? Yes, he had once heard a rumor that the man was called the Martial Hero.

“You crazy bitch!”

For the first time since meeting her, he cursed.

But she did not get angry. Instead, she laughed brightly, like a madwoman.

“That is why you must go mad too.”

Cha Iran slowly reached her hand toward An Cheonggwang’s neck. Her fingers moved along the red line drawn across his throat, then stopped at the end.

“That Young Cult Leader will not go easy on you the way I did.”

* * *

“There is someone I would like to introduce to you, miss.”

In the end, An Hupyeong took Ian to the reception hall.

All he wanted was to make himself look good in her eyes somehow.

—If you let a contestant meet someone judging the participants, it will cause trouble later.

Sachu tried to dissuade him through sound transmission, but it did not work.

—What trouble? I’ll say we happened to meet while passing by.

Sachu absolutely did not want to go to the reception hall. He did not want to see those frightening, bizarre people again, nor did he want to become further entangled with them.

—If the Gang Leader finds out, he will be furious.

But even that attempt to dissuade him did not work against, If it’s for this woman, I can bear anything.

—Fine. He’ll know if you go and tattle.

In the end, unable to break his stubbornness, they arrived at the reception hall where the Mistress of Cheonhwaru was staying.

When they entered the reception hall, the Cheonhwaru Mistress and her party were not relaxing comfortably as they had been last time.

Had they seen them coming through the window?

The Cheonhwaru Mistress was standing, and all the other guards were standing in a line as well. They looked as if they were observing proper etiquette to welcome an honored guest.

“Welcome.”

At the Cheonhwaru Mistress’s greeting, Geugak Soma and the Faceless Guests standing behind her all bowed at once.

At that extremely courteous reception, An Hupyeong felt as if he might fly. These people had not originally been this polite.

‘Yes, this is how it should have been from the start. These bastards have finally come to their senses.’

With his dignity restored in front of Ian, An Hupyeong puffed out his chest and spoke heartily.

“Have you all been well?”

How could he have known? That their courteous greeting was directed at Geom Mugeuk, who stood behind him.

At that moment, Sachu was watching to see how these two guards, who somehow gave off a similar feeling, would treat each other when they met.

‘They’re smiling?’

Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma were looking at each other and smiling.

‘Do madmen recognize each other?’

It did not occur to him that they might actually know each other.

Just then, Sachu’s eyes met those of one of the Faceless Guests.

Was it the fellow who had stared at him from beside him before? Since they were wearing veils, they all looked so similar that he could not tell.

Sachu naturally turned his gaze to the opposite side, pretending not to have seen him, then looked elsewhere. But on the side he turned to, another Faceless Guest was staring straight at him.

‘No, why do they keep looking at me!’

In the end, Sachu lowered his head.

‘Don’t look at me! Please don’t look at me!’

Then he stealthily lifted his head, only to find them still staring.

‘I didn’t tell anyone that Master Yong died. I won’t say anything in the future either!’

While Sachu was waging a desperate battle to find somewhere to put his eyes, An Hupyeong was in high spirits.

“This is Miss Ian, my honored guest. I was showing her around our gang and stopped by briefly as we were passing. This is the Mistress of Cheonhwaru, who will be serving as a judge in this competition.”

I’m the sort of person who can even introduce you to a judge of the competition!

An Hupyeong’s shoulders soared to the heavens.

Even though she had been told this person was judging the competition, Ian did not seem particularly uncomfortable.

“I’m Ian.”

An Hupyeong liked even that about her.

‘As expected of a member of the Demon Cult, she has such a straightforward personality.’

If she had started saying insincere things like, Why did you let me meet a judge? I want to become the greatest beauty under heaven fair and square, it would have truly annoyed him.

Naturally, Ian and the Cheonhwaru Mistress acted as if they were meeting for the first time.

“Are you perhaps participating in this competition?”

An Hupyeong answered in Ian’s stead.

“That’s right. Miss Ian passed the preliminaries. Ah, the two of you will be seeing each other in the finals, then.”

The Cheonhwaru Mistress’s expression stiffened slightly. Of course, it was an act performed in front of An Hupyeong. It would have been strange for her to accept such a situation as only natural.

As if the situation made her uncomfortable, the Cheonhwaru Mistress dismissed those around her.

“I would like to speak quietly with our guests, so all the guards, please wait outside.”

At that, Geugak Soma and the Faceless Guests immediately went outside.

Ian looked back at Geom Mugeuk and said,

“Please wait outside.”

Ian could tell that the reason the Cheonhwaru Mistress had sent Geugak Soma out was to give him a moment to be with Geom Mugeuk. Otherwise, there would have been no need to send him out.

Geom Mugeuk also went outside without a word.

Then An Hupyeong looked at Sachu.

“What are you doing not going out?”

Sachu was inwardly flustered, having never imagined that he would be made to leave too.

‘You want me to be out there with them? Please!’

But when the women had all sent out their guards, An Hupyeong was not the sort to leave only his own guard behind like a coward.

In the end, only after being scolded through sound transmission was Sachu driven outside as well.

In the yard, Geom Mugeuk, Geugak Soma, and the Faceless Guests stood looking at him.

He had the dignity of an unorthodox martial artist to uphold!

No, he did not. Sachu needlessly looked up at the sky and avoided their gazes.

At that moment, Geugak Soma strode toward him.

‘Why? Why are you coming over!’

Geugak Soma smiled and patted the terrified Sachu on the shoulder.

Just as Sachu relaxed, realizing it was meant as thanks for keeping the secret—

Swish.

Before he knew it, his acupoint had been sealed. He fell asleep and collapsed backward.

The Faceless Guests watched the surroundings on all sides, and in the center, Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma conversed.

Geom Mugeuk had been planning to come secretly that night to meet Geugak Soma anyway, but instead, he had been able to come openly under the guidance of the young gang leader.

They did not have much time, so Geom Mugeuk immediately said what needed to be said.

“The one behind this is a female assassin named Cha Iran.”

Geom Mugeuk also told him about the female assassins under her command. The Faceless Guests all listened as well.

“They do not possess the distinctive aura of assassins, so you must be especially careful.”

Geugak Soma, and of course the Faceless Guests as well, nodded.

After hearing about them, one question naturally occurred to Geugak Soma.

“If you know who the enemy is, why not eliminate them? The Pavilion Mistress’s prophecy took place on the stage. Then…”

“You mean we should eliminate them all before the finals are held?”

Geugak Soma nodded. He did not show it outwardly, but the prophecy had continued to weigh on his mind. It concerned the life of none other than Geom Mugeuk.

“Why are you not doing so?”

As if he had already considered the same thing, Geom Mugeuk revealed the reason.

“There are two reasons. The first is that if there is someone else behind them, I want to take care of that person as well.”

Killing the leader of the Sado Alliance was no ordinary conspiracy. Another hidden mastermind might reveal themselves. Just as in the Murim Alliance, there had been not only Cheonae Geosa but also So Jeongrak of Myeongsim Clinic.

If they could not deal with them all at once when they had the chance, it would one day return as another danger.

“In truth, the second reason troubles me more.”

Geom Mugeuk’s gaze deepened as he raised a fundamental question.

“Why are they trying to kill the leader of the Sado Alliance?”

Of course, it could have been a plan prepared long ago. Just as they had prepared for decades in the Murim Alliance, they might have planned to draw in Shinhwa Gang and kill the leader of the Sado Alliance.

But to kill the Sado Alliance leader now, when the Murim Alliance had failed? Even when they must know that he himself had intervened?

“Even if this plan succeeds, Young Alliance Leader Bi will inherit that position. What benefit could there possibly be for them to push this plan forward?”

Because of such doubts, he had not moved rashly.

“They may have another plan.”

Geugak Soma nodded as if in complete agreement. As expected, the Young Cult Leader saw through this situation far more deeply than he had imagined.

“What do you intend to do now?”

Geom Mugeuk smiled and answered.

“It is a prophecy handed down by none other than our Pavilion Mistress. It should not turn out to be wrong, should it? Will you go up onto the stage with me?”

How could Geugak Soma not know that this did not mean he intended to obediently be stabbed by a dagger?

Knowing that it meant they would stand proudly against fate, Geugak Soma’s eyes smiled brightly.

“I will be with you until the end.”

The Faceless Guests around them listened to the conversation in silence.

The smiles in their eyes resembled Geugak Soma’s smile, which said he would be with him until the end.

Just then, An Hupyeong’s voice came from inside the building, telling them to come in now.

Geom Mugeuk and Geugak Soma entered first.

The Faceless Guests woke Sachu, who had fallen asleep after having his acupoint sealed.

When Sachu, lying on the ground, opened his eyes, the Faceless Guests were crouched around him, looking down with concern.

“!”

Sachu closed his eyes again.

He wished it had been a dream, but from inside the building came An Hupyeong’s shout, asking what he was doing not coming in.

* * *

Bi Sain entered the training ground.

This was the personal training ground of the Sado Alliance leader, Baek Jagang.

“You called for me?”

Baek Jagang was carefully wiping down an iron man dummy made of ten-thousand-year cold iron.

This object, made for the leader of the Sado Alliance, bore countless sword marks.

“I know when and how each of these was made.”

With that alone, one could understand Baek Jagang’s realm, and the state of mind with which he swung his sword, stroke by stroke.

“Whenever I wanted to surpass someone, several people would overlap atop this iron man dummy. The one who appeared most often was the Demon Cult Leader.”

Bi Sain could tell. His master had truly wanted to surpass Geom Ujin. And seeing his master speak of it so comfortably made him think that perhaps his master had crossed into some new realm.

“I wonder who it will be in your life.”

Unexpected words flowed from Baek Jagang’s mouth.

“I intend to pass this down to you now.”

Bi Sain was startled.

“Alliance Leader!”

“It was meant to be passed down to you from the start. Take it and train well.”

Bi Sain knew better than anyone what this iron man dummy meant to Baek Jagang. Passing down this iron man dummy meant that he truly cherished and acknowledged him.

Bi Sain did not refuse. He respectfully clasped his fist and bowed at the waist.

“I will absolutely bring the Overlord Heaven-Shaking Sword Art to completion. I will absolutely become stronger.”

His powerful resolve was imbued with his emotion.

Baek Jagang nodded with a reassuring smile.

At that moment, a subordinate’s voice came from outside.

“The Young Cult Leader of the Divine Cult has sent a letter.”

At that news, Baek Jagang and Bi Sain looked at each other.

Because no one was allowed into the training ground, Bi Sain went out and received the letter.

Bi Sain read it.

“For what reason did he send it?”

“It concerns the selection competition for the Greatest Beauty Under Heaven.”

The two of them were well aware of the Greatest Beauty Under Heaven selection competition being held in Anhui.

Previously, the leader of Shinhwa Gang had personally come to ask permission, saying that he intended to hold such a competition and wished to offer, as its prize, a banquet attended by the leader of the Sado Alliance.

Shinhwa Gang was a sect that had long worked hard for the Sado Alliance, and even after its recent growth and rise in influence, its loyalty had not changed.

So permission had been granted, and now Geom Mugeuk had sent a letter concerning that matter.

After checking the letter, Bi Sain’s expression hardened, his gaze turning grave.

“It says there is a plan to assassinate you at that banquet, Alliance Leader.”

Since the letter had been sent by Geom Mugeuk, its contents would not be wrong. It even mentioned who the enemy was.

“It says she is a female assassin of the highest skill, and that she leads an assassination organization made up of women.”

Even though it was an assassination plan aimed at killing him, Baek Jagang instead seemed pleased.

He had felt strangely left out when he had previously received reports about what had happened in the Murim Alliance.

Those bastards only target the Murim Alliance leader? Are they looking down on me?

Bi Sain lowered his head with an ashamed expression.

“I apologize for having you hear such important information through the Young Cult Leader.”

This, too, was not Bi Sain’s responsibility. If one had to assign blame, punishment should be given to those who handled intelligence.

Baek Jagang did not mind at all, nor was he angry.

“At first, I thought the Young Cult Leader was simply outstanding and had gotten ahead of us. But after experiencing it myself, I realized fate has its own way of treating the Young Cult Leader. So do not concern yourself with it.”

His words implied that the ties between the force behind these events and Geom Mugeuk ran deep.

“So what did he say?”

“He repeatedly urged that you must not attend this banquet.”

But Baek Jagang had no intention whatsoever of being absent.

“If I, the protagonist of a conspiracy to kill me, do not go, would that do?”

Bi Sain knew that his master was not someone who would ever back down. Moreover, if Geom Mugeuk was stopping that matter in his stead? His master was not the kind of man to leave his own affairs to another.

“However, the Young Cult Leader sent two letters.”

“Two?”

“One is the official letter I just mentioned, and the other was addressed to me.”

Bi Sain checked the letter on the spot. After confirming its contents, he held it out to Baek Jagang. Baek Jagang wondered why he was showing him a personal letter.

After checking the contents, Baek Jagang’s small eyes disappeared with a great laugh.

—The Alliance Leader isn’t someone who won’t come just because it’s dangerous, is he? If he’s going to come anyway, bring him early!

Chapter 692: Aren’t You All Just That Foolish?
----------------------------------------------
After bidding farewell to the Master of Cheonhwaru and stepping outside, An Hupyeong brought out the words he had been saving.

“Now then, let us go to my place for a drink.”

Had he not exchanged those meaningless words with the Master of Cheonhwaru solely to say this to I An?

Since he had sent word in advance, good liquor and food would be prepared. And if he called for musicians to play a pleasant tune on top of that? She couldn’t possibly refuse!

An Hupyeong had naturally assumed that I An would agree.

He had introduced her to the examiner, so he had thought she would try to look good for him even a little.

“Let’s do it next time.”

I An refused point-blank.

“If you dislike alcohol, we can have tea instead.”

“Next time.”

Never before had a woman rejected him so openly, so An Hupyeong was flustered.

With any other woman, he would have exploded in anger, but this time he did not.

She was also a figure of the demonic path, and because he truly liked her, he still wanted to make a good impression on her.

As he stood there wondering how to react, I An approached him. When she drew near, a pleasant scent wafted from her.

The way she whispered as if only for him to hear was wonderful too.

“When we meet next time, tell me the real reason you held the competition. I simply can’t stand being curious.”

After saying that, I An lightly bowed her head in greeting and moved on. Geom Mugeuk walked after her.

‘That bastard! I want to smash him!’

Though the misplaced sparks of rage landed on the innocent Geom Mugeuk, the fire disappeared from sight before it could even ignite.

Then the only one left to him was Sachu.

Sachu stood blankly, staring at the guest hall.

Wondering what he was staring at, An Hupyeong looked too. Faceless Guests were gazing this way from the window.

“What? Did you lend money to those guys or something?”

The dazed Sachu finally came to his senses.

“No.”

“Then why are you looking at them like that?”

In truth, Sachu had been recalling what happened in the courtyard earlier.

When he had woken up after falling asleep, one of the Faceless Guests had extended a hand to him as he lay there.

He felt he would die if he didn’t take it, so he grabbed the hand and stood up.

‘Now I’ve truly joined hands with them.’

Until then, he had been too afraid to speak, but now he felt as if he were on their side.

The feeling that he had to keep the secret even more tightly was an added burden.

“What’s with you lately? Are you excited because you saw a pretty woman after so long?”

“No.”

He wished it were really that. Right now, no women were entering Sachu’s eyes.

As the only person who knew that Teacher Yong had died at their hands, he was in a silent battle of life and death.

“But where are you going?”

“To my father.”

Judging by his mood, An Hupyeong was now headed to the Lord to demand answers. Asking just what had prompted him to hold this competition.

All to tell I An the reason. He was truly acting like a madman over a woman.

“You cannot. The Lord will fly into a rage!”

As Sachu blocked his path, An Hupyeong yelled.

“Are you crazy?”

Sachu hurriedly stepped aside. It was certainly unlike his usual behavior.

“What’s wrong? Since when do you worry about me?”

Yes, he had always followed An Hupyeong, protecting him. Whatever he did, whatever nonsense he spouted, Sachu had merely guarded him.

Yet without realizing it, he had become invested in An Hupyeong’s affairs. Never once had he been a main player in this life, yet somehow he kept feeling the need to step forward.

“My apologies.”

Thus An Hupyeong went to the great hall where his father was.

Contrary to Sachu’s worries, An Cheongwang was not so angry.

“Were you that curious?”

An Hupyeong was inwardly flustered by his father’s calm demeanor.

He had expected his father to explode, yelling that it was none of his business. He had steeled himself for a severe scolding.

“Yes, I heard you changed the examiner at the last minute.”

An Cheongwang answered calmly.

“There were circumstances.”

And An Cheongwang had a guess as to why his son was interested in this matter.

“It is not what you are thinking, so do not trouble yourself with needless worry.”

The father he knew would never say such things. No, he would have already roared, asking if he wasn’t told to mind his own business. He had come prepared to be struck.

If so, he would have something to tell her instead. Saying how hard he had tried. He was enjoying this pure heart he held, for the first time in a long while—or rather, for the first time ever.

But his father was treating him calmly. So much so that it was bewildering.

“I will tell you the full story later.”

An Hupyeong was moved by his father’s gentle gaze.

Never once had his father treated him with such kind words. Because he had always looked at him with disappointment.

At least for this moment, he forgot I An. Even if he hadn’t gotten the words to tell her, it was all right.

“Thank you for telling me, Father.”

An Hupyeong bowed politely and went outside.

The soft expression vanished from An Cheongwang’s face as he watched his retreating son’s back.

His attitude softens and he backs down so easily? He is moved so easily?

Watching his son so nakedly reveal his inner thoughts made a sigh escape him.

Then a woman’s voice came from behind.

“A man in love cannot hide his true feelings.”

The one who appeared silently was Chairan.

An Cheongwang looked at her with calm eyes.

“You are the same.”

When he learned that the Young Cult Leader had gotten involved, he had cursed her as a crazy bitch.

Now, he had calmed his heart.

“You too cannot hide that you are hiding something from me. What are you obsessed with?”

Chairan smiled instead of answering and asked.

“Do you regret joining hands with me?”

He did not.

One night, he opened his eyes from sleep to find her looking down at him. Had she wanted to kill him, he would already be dead. She looked down at him and spoke.

—If you refuse my proposal, I will go to the Heitianmen.

The Heitianmen was the largest and strongest sect in Anhui.

An Cheongwang had greatly expanded the Sinhwabang, but he could not surpass the Heitianmen.

He could not win. No matter how hard he tried, he could not accomplish what the Leader of Heitianmen could. He felt the difference in innate ability and capacity. The Leader of Heitianmen was a wall. An absolute, unbreakable wall.

An Cheongwang replied to her words.

—Do not go.

The woman’s proposal had been truly astounding.

Host the competition for the World’s Greatest Beauty at Sinhwabang and invite the Leader of the Four Paths Alliance there, then eliminate him and pin the crime on the Leader of Heitianmen.

He accepted the proposal without much deliberation.

He had lived a life unscrupulous of means and methods from the start, and it was his dream to topple the Heitianmen, become the foremost faction of the Four Paths in Anhui, and eventually become the Foremost Faction of the Four Paths.

And above all, if he refused, this woman would go to the Heitianmen and make the same proposal. No, she wouldn’t even need to go. She would have come knowing he would accept. She would have caught the scent of the desire he had harbored for so long.

“Do not touch the Young Cult Leader.”

“You accepted killing the Leader of the Four Paths Alliance, so what are you afraid of?”

At first glance, the involvement of the demonic cult’s Young Cult Leader might seem like a good thing.

They would think the demonic cult was involved in the death of the Leader of the Four Paths Alliance. The fact that he had helped would be buried forever in that chaos.

If only for this one case.

If the Young Cult Leader dies?

Would the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult leave those involved alone? Wouldn’t the order come down to kill everyone related, guilty or not? The Heavenly Demon Divine Cult he knew would surely do so. They would try to annihilate the Sinhwabang for the sole reason of having hosted the competition.

“Killing the Young Cult Leader is absolutely forbidden.”

“I won’t kill him.”

“It must absolutely not happen!”

Despite his repeated dissuasion, Chairan vanished from before his eyes, leaving behind deliberately spiteful words.

“You keep acting like that, so now I really want to kill him.”

* * *

Chairan stood alone in a field under a brightly shining moon.

The grass swaying in the wind seemed almost to breathe.

A man walked from afar, cutting through that breath.

He was a man who had dressed to the nines. Expensive clothes and accessories, and even a folding fan painted with beauties.

Moreover, he wore thick makeup on his face; the white makeup was excessive. His individuality was so extreme that it felt like self-torture. Because of that, the man’s face could not be made out clearly.

The man walked up to her, fluttering his fan.

“It’s been a while, friend.”

Chairan made an expression of surprise at the man’s greeting.

“I didn’t think you would bring it personally.”

“I said I would bring it. Just wanted to see your face.”

The man’s name was Hua Daoming.

The two were an unlikely pair, yet they were friends who got along surprisingly well. Perhaps that was why? Chairan’s expression was more comfortable than usual.

“You came to see the Young Cult Leader, not me.”

Hua Daoming admitted readily.

“A matter this entertaining is rare in a lifetime. Are you really going to kill the Young Cult Leader, too?”

An Cheongwang’s intuition had been correct. Her claim that she would not kill the Young Cult Leader was a lie.

“The Leader of the Four Paths Alliance and the Young Cult Leader. Doing it alone will be too much, no?”

“Yet it was an order given knowing that, was it not?”

“That sounds as though I gave the order.”

“At the very least, you could have stopped it.”

Hua Daoming fanned himself quickly, as if playing dumb.

“As you know, things are complicated on this side as well.”

Chairan made an expression showing she did not particularly want to understand.

“You’ve met the Young Cult Leader?”

When she nodded, Hua Daoming asked quickly.

“How was it? Is he really as incredible as the rumors say?”

“If you’re curious, meet him yourself.”

“Not curious enough to risk my life. I came here solely as an errand runner and spectator.”

“So there is someone who can make you afraid.”

Then Hua Daoming mentioned someone unexpected.

“You haven’t heard the story? When Agunhak returned...”

At the mention of him, the One King and Sword King, Chairan’s eyes sparkled slightly.

The only person on their side whom Chairan felt any liking for was Agunhak.

She always emphasized that it was not romantic feeling, but the fondness of a martial artist and a human being.

And the only one who knew that fact was Hua Daoming.

“The higher-ups interrogated him. Why did he return without killing the Young Cult Leader? Do you know what he answered?”

“What did he say?”

“He said this.”

He cleared his throat and imitated Agunhak’s words.

“It was not I who spared him. The Young Cult Leader saved me.”

At those words, Chairan’s eyes widened.

“How could that be?”

She made an expression of utter disbelief. She could not imagine the Agunhak she knew losing to someone.

“Right, with his skill, he couldn’t have lost to the Young Cult Leader. But he really said that. I was there.”

Chairan recalled the Young Cult Leader. She had not yet exchanged proper words with Geom Mugeuk, so she could not know what kind of person he was. She knew he was strong, but even so. That he had saved Agunhak? She could not believe it.

“There must have been circumstances.”

“That’s not the only surprising thing.”

At least for Chairan, what came next was even more astonishing.

“Agunhak declared then and there that he would leave the organization. It was truly a sight to behold. He said he brought a chest, so his debt to you is fully repaid.”

Chairan’s expression shifted through various emotions. The first thought that came to her was.

“That is so like him.”

She understood his resolve.

“If even he leaves, only trash like us will remain.”

She also reproached herself.

“The higher-ups wouldn’t have permitted it?”

She also had doubts.

Hua Daoming silently watched her emotions change quickly and complicatedly, unlike her usual self. It was a moment that reaffirmed Agunhak was the only one who could shake her.

“Why think that? Do you believe we are foolish enough to make Agunhak an enemy?”

At that, Chairan nodded.

“Aren’t you all just that foolish?”

At her words, Hua Daoming laughed aloud.

But Chairan did not laugh. If Agunhak left safely, it would be regrettable; if the organization captured him, it would also be a problem. Perhaps the order to kill him would come down to her.

Hua Daoming took something from his bosom.

“But we aren’t complete fools. We finally created this.”

What he took out was a small box.

“Will this turn the tide?”

He asked, offering the box to Chairan.

“Confident?”

“If I’m not?”

“I’ll take your place.”

Beneath the thick makeup, Hua Daoming’s eyes turned coldly still. He felt completely different from when he had been fluttering his fan.

Chairan quietly took the box.

“If you came to watch, then just watch. The Young Cult Leader will die by my hands.”

“If you say so, then so it shall be.”

Hua Daoming said, turning around.

“I shall enjoy the show!”

Smiling, Hua Daoming fanned himself as he walked away lightly.

Chairan, watching his retreating figure from afar, slowly opened the box.

Inside the box was a single red bead.

Chapter 693: Life Is Short but Eyelashes Should Be Long
-------------------------------------------------------
Cha Iran lifted the box and peered at the bead.

The surface of the bead was smooth, and the red light suffusing it was clear yet deep.

Looking closer, she saw that inside the bead, things like a person’s blood vessels stretched out like a web, and around them some sort of energy shimmered and moved.

Was it because it felt as though it were alive?

It felt not as if she were looking into it, but as if the bead were looking at her.

Just as Cha Iran carefully closed the lid of the box, someone appeared behind her.

The person who emerged was a woman dressed in black martial attire. She was Cha Iran’s hands and feet, and Ilgyo, the most skilled member of the assassin organization Gaingyo.

She, too, was beautiful, and had indeed passed the preliminaries of this competition.

After bowing respectfully, Ilgyo reported.

“The Sado Alliance Leader has departed from the Sado Alliance. At my estimation, he will likely arrive on the day the main round is held.”

Cha Iran tilted her head slightly.

“That is earlier than expected.”

They would select the eight who would advance to the final match from the main round, and three days after that, the finals would be held.

The banquet attended by the Sado Alliance Leader would be held on the evening after the finals.

And yet he would arrive not for the finals, but on the day of the main round?

“It seems he intends to watch the main round of this competition.”

After the Young Cult Leader of the Demonic Cult became involved, many things had begun to change. For the side intending to carry out the plan, it was not a welcome development.

“The escort?”

“As expected, the Alliance Leader Hall’s guard unit and the Young Alliance Leader have set out with the Thirteen Wolves of Sado.”

Cha Iran nodded.

“What about the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion?”

“He remains in the guest hall.”

“And the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon?”

“He has not moved.”

Cha Iran spoke to Ilgyo as if warning her.

“They are bizarre and strange, so it is easy to let down one’s guard. But do not forget. The words ‘Extreme Evil’ are attached before their names.”

Ilgyo lowered her head, her gaze sharp.

“Yes! I will keep it in mind.”

After bowing respectfully, Ilgyo looked at her leader.

She knew better than anyone what an outstanding assassin Cha Iran was, but the enemies they were facing this time were monstrous. They were more splendid than the dazzling clothes she was wearing now.

The Young Cult Leader, the Extreme Evil Lesser Demon, and the Sado Alliance Leader and Young Alliance Leader.

Reading the worry in Ilgyo’s eyes, Cha Iran held out the box she was carrying.

“Do not worry. The reason we will win is inside here.”

The box was firmly shut, but an unknowable, ominous aura wavered around it.

* * *

At last, the day of the main round of the World’s Greatest Beauty Selection Competition dawned.

Yi An had risen before daybreak and climbed the back mountain to train in martial arts.

Geom Mugeuk followed and watched her as she trained, sweat running down her body. There were times when they inevitably had to be apart, but he was trying not to leave Yi An’s side if he could help it.

“I’m just too nervous.”

She felt most at ease when she was training in martial arts.

After she finished a round of training, Geom Mugeuk asked her,

“Do you feel a bit better now?”

“I feel like I can breathe a little.”

She said that, but she was still trembling. It was nerve-racking enough to display martial arts in front of countless people, and now she had to show off her beauty on top of that.

Geom Mugeuk understood her heart better than anyone.

“I’m sorry.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, Yi An smiled.

“I’m the one who should be grateful. If not for you, Young Master, when would I ever get to live such an interesting life?”

“Right? Exactly?”

Instead of agreeing, Yi An lowered her head deeply and started walking.

“As if. I want to run away even now.”

Geom Mugeuk laughed and followed after her.

When the two returned to the inn, a woman was waiting for her.

Surprisingly, she was someone Geom Mugeuk had called.

“I asked them to send this lady here.”

The woman had been found through inquiries at the Anhui branch of the Heavenly Demon Divine Cult. She was proficient in makeup and had come to do Yi An’s makeup.

“You can’t go to the main round bare-faced, can you?”

Yi An was truly grateful that Geom Mugeuk had thought even this far ahead. As it happened, she had been worrying about how she should do her makeup.

After washing clean and sitting before the bronze mirror, Yi An heard the woman say with a smile,

“Life may be short, but one’s eyelashes should be long.”

To this woman, makeup was life itself. It held the same meaning to her that a sword did to a martial artist.

She advised Yi An to wear the white court gown today out of the two outfits she had bought.

As she applied makeup to match the clothes, she explained the techniques in detail. It was truly makeup artistry being passed down by a woman of the highest skill.

While she did Yi An’s makeup, Geom Mugeuk closed his eyes, circulating his qi and regulating his breathing as he steadied his internal energy and his mind. Now the main round stage was beginning.

He could not know when the scene the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion had witnessed would occur. It might happen today, or it might be on the final stage three days later.

He felt sorry for the Master of Cheonhwa Pavilion, but that event would not occur.

The makeup did not take as long as expected.

She personally demonstrated that a true master did not drag out her work. No, she demonstrated it with Yi An’s face.

A light tap here, a swift touch there. Her hands moved so lightly that one wondered if it was all right. If this woman’s makeup artistry were compared to martial arts, it would be a swift sword technique.

“There, it’s done.”

After the makeup was finished, Yi An turned toward Geom Mugeuk. The corners of her tense eyes trembled faintly.

Even she could not adjust to the reflection in the bronze mirror. She could not judge whether she was prettier than before, so she was both worried and expectant about what Geom Mugeuk would say.

Geom Mugeuk, who had been circulating his qi with his eyes closed, slowly opened them.

Looking silently at Yi An, Geom Mugeuk said something she could not understand.

“Live with that face from now on.”

“You want me to wear makeup every day?”

Since it sounded like he was saying, Your face with makeup is prettier than your bare face! Yi An turned to the woman who had done her makeup and joked,

“Your skills are too good. You’ve become my hostage.”

Of course, she sensed that Geom Mugeuk’s words had another meaning. He was not the sort of person who would tell her, someone who lived absorbed in martial arts, to put on makeup every day.

Indeed, there was a hidden meaning in Geom Mugeuk’s words.

“That’s not what I mean. I’m telling you to live with the feeling this made-up face gives off.”

If the Yi An without makeup was purity itself, then the Yi An with makeup looked intense. It was difficult to find the right words for it, yet she was beautiful. There was certainly an astonishing power in a woman’s makeup.

“Don’t live with such a soft heart. Don’t live being too kind. Don’t be the first to speak to people, and if someone approaches you, live as if you’re saying, Get lost!”

You can’t even live like that yourself, Young Master!

Because she knew well what kind of heart Geom Mugeuk was speaking from, Yi An smiled.

“Yes! If I feel like my heart is weakening, I’ll start with makeup.”

“Then shall we go?”

Before leaving, Yi An respectfully expressed her gratitude to the woman.

“Thank you so much.”

“I already received a large sum, so I am the one who should be grateful. If you advance to the finals, I will see you then.”

“Then today might be the last time.”

Yi An’s humility did not work on the woman.

“In three days, I will teach you makeup artistry suited to a red court gown. Look forward to it!”

The woman naturally believed Yi An would make it to the finals. She had done makeup for countless women, but this was the first time she had seen a woman this beautiful, and also the first time she had felt so rewarded after applying makeup. She truly wanted to be dragged away as a hostage and do her makeup every day.

And so Geom Mugeuk and Yi An headed to Sinhwabang.

The streets were packed with people. Yi An moved with a veiled bamboo hat on and a thin cloak covering her clothes.

Since more than a hundred women had advanced to the main round, today’s main round stage would take quite some time.

Behind the main round stage was the largest hall in Sinhwabang, and that was the waiting room where the participants waited.

Guards were not allowed to enter that waiting room.

“Yi An.”

“Yes, Young Master.”

“You know, don’t you? Who’s inside there.”

This time, the enemy was said to be an assassin organization made up of beautiful women. That meant not only Cha Iran, but several other assassins might be inside as well. No, they certainly would be.

“The only one you can trust now is yourself.”

Yi An silently nodded.

Just then, someone walked over. It was none other than An Hupyeong. Sachu and the martial artists lined up some distance away.

“Miss Yi. I hope you achieve good results.”

“Thank you.”

“You will definitely pass the main round. I guarantee it.”

His words, saying he guaranteed it, sounded as if he meant he would use his influence to make her pass.

He did not know that such words were discourteous to the other person and would instead lose him points. He had lived his whole life thinking only from his own perspective, so he had never been trained to consider another person’s feelings.

His father, An Cheonggwang, had built Sinhwabang into a great and powerful force, but the price of that devotion had to be paid in the place closest to him.

Yi An did not speak with him any further and entered the hall.

It would have been nice if she had said a few more words. Feeling pointlessly disappointed and spiteful, An Hupyeong glared at Geom Mugeuk.

A fellow he disliked every time he saw him.

A fellow he hated for no real reason.

A fellow he wanted to drag away, beat up, and frighten badly.

That was why the words that came out of his mouth could not be pleasant.

“Guard Miss Yi properly! Understand?”

Because he had lived his whole life being guarded, he had always looked down on those who served as guards.

“Yes.”

His petty spite continued as a question.

“How long have you been guarding Miss Yi?”

“A long time.”

“So when did you start!”

When had Yi An started guarding him?

“Since we were little.”

“Little?”

For a moment, An Hupyeong felt puzzled.

She had been guarded since childhood? That meant she was of noble status, did it not?

“Miss Yi… what is her position in the Divine Cult?”

Geom Mugeuk looked at An Hupyeong with a faint smile.

It felt as if he were sneering, asking why he was only asking that now, and An Hupyeong’s expression hardened.

‘This bastard is laughing?’

Just as he was wondering how to thoroughly crush him,

a woman walked over.

Seeing her, An Hupyeong was startled. A woman as beautiful as Yi An was walking toward them. The beauty who gave off a completely different impression was none other than Cha Iran.

“It seems Miss Yi has already gone in.”

“She just entered.”

“Then an opportunity has come to me.”

When he saw Cha Iran smile alluringly at Geom Mugeuk, An Hupyeong’s eyes narrowed.

From what he could tell, she was clearly a woman who knew Yi An, yet merely because he was a guard martial artist, he was conversing with such a beauty.

“How about it? Shall we sneak off for a meal?”

As usual, she was tempting him as if joking, but Cha Iran’s feelings as she looked at Geom Mugeuk were clearly different from before.

‘You saved that man? I can’t believe it.’

Ak Gunhak must have spared him. He was not someone who listened to every order blindly. That was why she had liked him.

So she wanted to ask Geom Mugeuk. What had happened between him and that man? Why was he trying to leave? And, if by any chance he knew, where he intended to go.

Ever since she had heard about him from Hwa Domyeong yesterday, it had been bothering her.

But the chance to hear about Ak Gunhak would likely not come unless it was just before this Young Cult Leader died.

“Tonight, let’s have a drink together to celebrate advancing to the finals.”

She was saying that she would naturally pass, and she thought Geom Mugeuk would, as usual, naturally refuse.

“Let’s do that.”

At Geom Mugeuk’s answer, Cha Iran gave him a look of surprise.

“Even if Miss Yi is eliminated, you must keep the promise.”

“Even if you are eliminated, do not be ashamed and come. I will buy you a drink.”

Cha Iran smiled and went inside. Until then, An Hupyeong had been looking only for a chance to cut in, but in the end, he could not speak to her.

If it had been the usual An Hupyeong, he would certainly have inserted himself into the conversation. Who might you be, miss? I am so-and-so. How pleasant to meet you like this.

But he could not say a word. It was not because of his single-minded devotion to Yi An.

He did not know why, but it was difficult to speak to her carelessly. The feeling she gave off was unmistakably like that. Do not interrupt me now!

“Who is that woman?”

“She is Miss Cha Iran.”

“Who are you to make a dinner appointment without Miss Yi’s permission? Who are you to say she should come even if she is eliminated and not be ashamed?”

Seeing him converse and even joke with a woman he himself had not managed to say a single word to, his jealousy exploded.

Truly, if not for Yi An, he would have ordered Sachu and his subordinates to drag him somewhere secluded and beat him half to death.

“From this moment on, don’t say a single word! The instant I see you open your mouth, I’ll tear that mouth apart!”

After threatening him like that, he walked toward where Sachu was.

Sachu, who happened to be receiving a report from a subordinate, spoke urgently.

“They say the Sado Alliance Leader and the Young Alliance Leader have arrived.”

“What? The Sado Alliance Leader is already here?”

An Hupyeong was greatly shocked. He did not know about anyone else, but he absolutely had to look good before the Sado Alliance Leader and the Young Alliance Leader.

“Where are they? Where are they now!”

“They are probably meeting the Gang Leader.”

“Hurry and send the men to find out. Especially if the Young Alliance Leader moves anywhere, tell them to contact me at once!”

The person more important to him was not the Sado Alliance Leader, but the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance. If he made a good impression on him, his life would be easier from here on.

“I have to be the first to meet him!”

“Understood.”

As he was flustering about, he saw Geom Mugeuk far off, speaking with someone. The other party appeared to be a young man, but his back was turned, so An Hupyeong could not tell who he was.

He had told him not to say a single word today, yet Geom Mugeuk was speaking with him, smiling brightly.

‘This bastard!’

An Hupyeong strode toward the two of them with Sachu and his subordinates.

“I told you I’d tear your mouth if you opened it, didn’t I?”

“I truly didn’t want to.”

Geom Mugeuk tattled on the man in front of him.

“This person forced me to speak.”

Chapter 694: What's with your face?
-----------------------------------
“Are you in the middle of making another heart?”

At Bisain’s voice from behind him, a smile formed on Geom Mugeuk’s lips. It seemed he had seen him talking with Cha Iran.

“Unfortunately, the new heart you saw is not one that beats inside my body.”

Geom Mugeuk turned around.

Originally, there had not been so much as a crack on Bisain’s terrifying face for even the faintest smile to slip into, but now there was room enough for a fairly large joy at seeing him.

He had come here on high alert, since this was a place where a plan to assassinate his master was in motion, but he could not hide the gladness he felt upon meeting Geom Mugeuk.

“When did you arrive?”

“Just now.”

“Of course it would be you.”

He wondered what he was about to say this time.

“The finals of the greatest beauty contest under heaven are about to begin, you see. How badly must the horses have been worked by your obsession with seeing beauties?”

It was not only horses that were dragged out for the sake of teasing Bisain. Even Han Seol, who had returned to the Northern Sea Ice Palace, was dragged into it.

“What do you think Miss Han would think if she knew you arrived exactly in time for the finals?”

Bisain was dumbfounded.

Who was it that had even sent him a letter telling him to come early if he was going to come anyway?

Seeing Geom Mugeuk begin teasing him the moment they met, he finally felt as though he had truly met him.

It was at that moment. Someone came walking up from behind and said,

“I told you I’d rip it if you opened your mouth, didn’t I?”

In that instant, Bisain saw it. The mischief that flashed across Geom Mugeuk’s face. Sure enough.

“I truly wasn’t going to, but this man forced me to do it.”

There was really no stopping Geom Mugeuk.

But putting that aside, not his eyes or his nose, but his mouth? He was going to rip Geom Mugeuk’s mouth? That mouth even Alliance Leaders could not handle!

Just who was this poor sacrifice, speaking such outrageous words without knowing who he was facing?

An Hupyeong’s voice came from behind him.

“Hey, is what he said true?”

If speaking to a friend out of gladness counted as forcing him to talk.

Bisain nodded obediently, like a man who had emptied his mind. He was playing along with Geom Mugeuk.

At the sight, An Hupyeong frowned.

He had asked a question, and this man only nodded without answering? Fine, all fellows who kept company with that detestable bodyguard were like this. He disliked him from the very beginning.

“Hey, turn around.”

Bisain slowly turned around.

When he saw the other man’s face, a curse burst from An Hupyeong’s mouth.

“Good grief, damn!”

It was not that he had meant to curse; he was simply so startled that the words popped out.

It was truly the first time in his life he had seen someone with such a frightening look.

Bisain’s face was covered in scars.

Slashed and twisted, so that merely looking at it sent a chill through one’s chest.

Unfortunately, the beauties entering the waiting room happened to see that scene. In the life of the arrogant An Hupyeong, showing himself intimidated in front of women was unthinkable.

“What’s wrong with your face? Did you fight every battle in the world all by yourself?”

Unfortunately, he failed to recognize who the other party was.

Good, he doesn’t recognize him! Geom Mugeuk said as though wronged.

“How could I not speak when such a frightening person struck up a conversation with me?”

The corner of Bisain’s mouth rose slightly.

He had grown used to Geom Mugeuk making jokes with his face.

He now also knew that this was Geom Mugeuk’s consideration. When others mocked his face, Geom Mugeuk mocked it with them, as if to say this:

Don’t mind it, my friend. Just enjoy it too!

“If you were me, Young Gang Leader, could you refuse to answer when that terrifying face spoke to you?”

Yes, if Geom Mugeuk did not play such jokes on him, he would probably feel disappointed instead.

At Geom Mugeuk’s words, An Hupyeong nodded. Hating the bodyguard was one thing, but he admitted that much was true. Even he would have answered.

“You, do you know that fellow?”

At An Hupyeong’s question, Bisain looked at Geom Mugeuk. Since he had referred to Geom Mugeuk as “that fellow,” this was a situation where he ought to pretend not to know.

Bisain shook his head as if he did not know him.

An Hupyeong still disliked the fact that he would not answer in words, but it was not easy to get angry while looking at that face.

“If you don’t know each other, why did you speak to him?”

Then Geom Mugeuk stepped forward in Bisain’s place.

“He came up to me and asked whether many beautiful women had arrived. He said he had ridden here with all his might because of this contest.”

An Hupyeong could not hold back and sneered.

“With that face, he lusts after women?”

At An Hupyeong’s words, the corners of Bisain’s eyes twitched. Feeling an inexplicable chill, An Hupyeong raised his shield.

“I am the Young Gang Leader of the Shinhwa Gang. The host of this contest.”

Then he asked Geom Mugeuk again.

“You were laughing while you talked.”

At An Hupyeong’s interrogation, Geom Mugeuk asked back as though wronged.

“Then should I laugh when looking at that face? How could I frown?”

An Hupyeong laughed aloud. Yes, that was true too. Yes, it would not be easy to frown while looking at that face.

“Hey, merciless face.”

As if calling him by a name he should never use were not enough, he even offered to hire him.

“I don’t know whose employ you’re in, but have you any thought of working under me?”

A fierce-looking martial artist speaking with Geom Mugeuk in front of the participants’ waiting room. Naturally, he assumed he was a bodyguard who had accompanied one of the contestants.

“I’ll pay you double.”

There was a reason he wanted to hire him.

If he went around with a frightening bastard like this at his side, it felt as though there would be nothing in the world to fear.

And one more thing.

An Hupyeong turned his head and looked back at Sachu behind him.

“My bodyguard has been rather unsatisfactory lately, you see.”

It was the same even now. If he had spoken, the man should show some kind of reaction, but once again, he was absentmindedly preoccupied with something else.

Sachu, for his part, found Bisain’s face oddly familiar and had been continuously trying to recall it. There was no way he would fail to remember a face like that.

The most frightening face he had ever seen belonged to the heir of the Sado Alliance. He had seen him from far away, and that intense face had never left his memory.

But the notion that the heir of the Sado Alliance would be conversing with this bodyguard in front of the waiting room had never entered his mind in the first place, so he never imagined the other man might be Bisain. Even though he had only just heard that the Young Alliance Leader had arrived today.

Ah, and that made it even less likely that he was the Young Alliance Leader. He would surely be exchanging greetings with the Gang Leader right now; there was no way he would be talking to that bodyguard here first.

“What are you doing right now!”

At An Hupyeong’s rebuke, Sachu snapped back to his senses.

“My apologies.”

“How many times has this been now! You’ve had many problems lately!”

When An Hupyeong glared at him with an angry face, Sachu bowed his head deeply.

‘Why does this keep happening to me lately?’

Just then, a martial artist came running over.

“The Gang Leader is urgently looking for you.”

Before leaving, An Hupyeong said to Bisain,

“If it is difficult for you to say it, I’ll personally tell the person you serve.”

An Hupyeong turned and walked away, and Sachu followed behind him.

“You stay behind and rip that fellow’s mouth!”

An Hupyeong took all his other subordinates and strode off.

Sachu knew he had not meant for him to really rip it.

The other party was a demon, and on top of that, he was the bodyguard of Yi An, whom An Hupyeong liked. If he really ripped it, there would be an uproar.

If he took the order literally, it was obvious he would be the one saddled with all the responsibility.

Sachu looked at Geom Mugeuk and warned him coldly.

“Watch that mouth of yours from now on! It really will get ripped!”

Geom Mugeuk feigned surprise and covered his mouth with his hand, while Bisain regretted that no one really could rip that mouth just once.

Watching Sachu walk away into the distance, Geom Mugeuk said,

“To think he would try to rip my mouth! The mouth of a man who makes his living by talking!”

Bisain laughed. He had only just arrived, and already he had lost count of how many times he had laughed. This was why being with Geom Mugeuk was enjoyable.

Just then, Illang walked over, greeted Geom Mugeuk, and reported to Bisain.

“The Alliance Leader is looking for you.”

He had come straight to meet Geom Mugeuk upon arriving, but now it was time to go greet the leader of the Shinhwa Gang.

“I’ll see you later.”

“I suppose I’ll have to pay my respects to the Alliance Leader later.”

It meant that, for now, he would stay as close to Yi An as possible. It also meant that the situation was not easy.

“I’ll speak well of you.”

“Thank you.”

After walking a few steps, Bisain looked back and said,

“Thank you for telling me about this matter.”

“We agreed to do that, didn’t we?”

If anything happened, they would be sure to tell each other.

Geom Mugeuk and Bisain looked at each other and nodded.

Meanwhile, Sachu, who had been walking away without strength, looked back.

‘No matter how much I think about it, he looks familiar.’

At that moment, Sachu noticed someone new.

There was one more person with the two of them. And that new person was someone Sachu knew as well.

‘Illang?’

Sachu recognized Illang. There had once been a time when his dream was to become one of the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance. He had eventually given up because he lacked the ability.

Because he had admired them so much, there had been a time when he guarded the main gate of the Sado Alliance just to catch a glimpse of their faces. He might fail to recognize others, but how could he not recognize Illang? But why was Illang here?

“Aaah!”

Sachu screamed aloud.

Then An Hupyeong, who was walking far ahead, looked back at him.

At the sight of him standing alone in the distance and screaming.

“That one really has gone mad lately. Completely mad.”

An Hupyeong shook his head and resumed walking.

Bisain and Illang walked toward where Sachu stood.

Sachu saw it.

Martial artists who had been hiding themselves somewhere began joining behind Bisain one by one and following him.

One, two, three……

The number that increased like that was thirteen in all. They were the very people he had wanted to become even in his dreams.

Bisain and the Thirteen Wolves of the Sado Alliance passed by Sachu, who stood completely frozen.

Bisain passed him without so much as giving him a glance.

He had wondered when he saw Illang, but now it was certain.

‘He really is the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance!’

Sachu stood in place, his body trembling. His master, who had always caused accidents, had finally caused a major one. It was strange that they had survived that situation at all.

Ah, there was a reason they had survived.

Bisain had not said a single word, but there had been someone who had said everything in his place.

His gaze turned toward Geom Mugeuk in the distance. He was still standing in front of the waiting room.

‘What are you?’

Who are you, that you conversed with the Young Alliance Leader of the Sado Alliance? Did you really meet him without knowing? Or did you meet him while knowing something?

‘Just what the hell are you?’

At that moment, his eyes met Geom Mugeuk’s.

Geom Mugeuk grinned at him, then used both hands to make a gesture of ripping his mouth wide open.

In that instant, Sachu could feel it. It was a warning telling him to simply keep quiet.

A warning that if he said anything useless, his mouth would be ripped apart.

Sachu whipped around and began walking.

Just then, he saw a group of people going out to the contest grounds in the distance. It was the Mistress of Cheonhwa Pavilion, followed by Geugak Soma and the Faceless Guests.

Sachu abruptly changed direction again and walked away.

‘Why is everyone doing this to me?’

* * *

A dozen or so carriages entered the inner courtyard of the Shinhwa Gang.

They were carriages reserved for the leader of the Sado Alliance, crafted so they could not be cut by ordinary blades and would not burn in fire.

Clack, clack, clack, clack, clack, clack!

The carriages stopped in a line, and their doors opened simultaneously as the Alliance Leader’s Hall guards stepped down. As befitting the finest guards of the Sado Alliance, their gazes were deep and sharp.

Once they descended and lined up before the carriage, the one carriage door that had not opened finally did.

The person who stepped down from the carriage was Baek Jagang, leader of the Sado Alliance.

Because he wore not splendid robes but ordinary martial attire, he stood out all the more. His presence was something no one else could imitate.

Before him, An Cheonggwang, leader of the Shinhwa Gang, came running and bowed at the waist.

“I greet the Alliance Leader.”

He truly had not known Baek Jagang would arrive today. Cha Iran had not told him that Baek Jagang would arrive early, so that he would naturally show surprise and confusion.

Since An Cheonggwang had no way of knowing the circumstances, he remained bowed and tense.

‘Why has he come early? Did he perhaps notice something?’

The busy leader of the Sado Alliance had no reason to come several days early.

“I did not know you would come this early.”

At that, Baek Jagang slowly approached him.
